While most models reliably identify distinct local vehicles, significant perceptual bottlenecks remain for long-tail, non-standard objects like stalls and railings. This research provides crucial empirical evidence and novel insights for model selection and synthetic data strategies, facilitating the practical deployment of autonomous driving perception systems in rural areas.

</details>


### [104] [ScratchSim: A Procedural Synthetic Data Pipeline for Surface Scratch Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27065)
*Paul Julius Kühn,Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Tiago Kleist,Richard Hoffmann,Arjan kuijper,Michael Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于BlenderProc的程序化渲染管线，用于生成大规模带标注的合成缺陷数据，并通过微调与混合训练策略，在轻量化边缘部署检测器上显著提升表面划痕检测性能，减少对真实标注数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业缺陷检测中，表面划痕等自动化检测至关重要，但标注缺陷数据稀缺，使得该任务极具挑战性。

Method: 利用BlenderProc构建程序化渲染管线，生成大规模带COCO格式标注的合成训练数据，支持可配置材质外观、相机模式和域随机化；评估四种训练策略（纯合成、纯真实、混合、从合成权重微调）在两种材质物体和三种轻量化检测器（YOLOX、YOLO26、LW-DETR）上的表现。

Result: 从合成权重微调始终优于纯真实训练；混合训练在真实数据稀缺条件下有效恢复性能；该结论在卷积和Transformer架构上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法无需大量真实标注数据集即可实现可扩展的缺陷检测，适用于端侧工业检测场景，并将在论文接收后开源相关资源。

Abstract: While automated defect detection such as the detection of surface scratched is an important aspect in industrial quality control, the scarcity of annotated defect data make this task challenging. This paper presents a procedural rendering pipeline that generates large-scale annotated synthetic training data using BlenderProc, with configurable material appearance, camera modes, and domain randomization, producing automatic COCO-format annotations. To show the potential of our approach, we evaluate four training strategies, namely synthetic-only, real-only, mixed, and fine-tuning from synthetic weights, across two objects with different material properties and three lightweight edge-deployable detectors, YOLOX, YOLO26, and LW-DETR. Our evaluation show that fine-tuning from synthetic weights consistently outperforms real-only training, and that mixed training effectively recovers performance under scarce real-data conditions, with findings validated across both convolutional and transformer-based architectures. The proposed approach enables scalable defect detection without the burden of large real annotated datasets, making it practical for on-device industrial inspection. The pipeline scripts, 3D model, and both synthetic and real annotated scratch datasets for a glossy toy Ferrari car will be made available through the project website upon acceptance.

</details>


### [105] [SciFigAlign: Scoring Scientific Figures by Fine-tuned Alignment of Visuals with Manuscript Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27066)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Zihan Deng,Huiqi Liang,Chengkun Yue,Zhanlin Cui,Pengfei Ye,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigAlign，一个面向同行评审场景的科学图表质量评估框架，通过微调CLIP和SciBERT，结合跨模态注意力与CubeMLP融合，在3857张标注图表上实现了比最佳LLM基线低59%的误差，揭示了科学图表评估需要视觉内容与稿件证据的深度对齐，而非单纯依赖提示工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像质量评估方法（IQA、CLIP、零样本LLM/VLM）在科学图表评估中存在根本性局限：IQA只能捕捉感知质量，CLIP缺乏对稿件上下文的理解，零样本方法评分集中且无法有效融合视觉与文本证据。科学图表评估需要同时考虑视觉清晰度、对论文主张的支持度以及信息层次表达，亟需一种能够将图表质量与稿件证据对齐的评估方法。

Method: 构建了包含3857张同行评审会议论文图表的标注数据集，沿清晰度(Clarity)、相关性(Relevance)、信息量(Informativeness)和结构(Structured)四个维度评分。提出SciFigAlign多模态评分器，输入包括图表截图、标题、引用段落和轻量论文上下文，采用端到端微调CLIP和SciBERT，通过逐模态交叉注意力(per-modality cross-attention)和CubeMLP融合，联合优化SmoothL1回归损失与同论文内排序铰链损失(within-paper ranking hinge loss)。

Result: 在按论文划分的测试集（n=396）上，SciFigAlign的宏观MAE为0.3524，同论文内成对准确率达81.64%，相比最佳LLM基线（MAE 0.864）实现59%的相对误差降低。消融实验证实：基于稿件的输入、引用上下文去噪和排序监督均至关重要。

Conclusion: 科学图表评估需要学习视觉内容与稿件证据之间的对齐，而非仅依靠提示工程，即使使用最先进的大视觉语言模型也无法替代这种结构化对齐方法。

Abstract: Scientific figure assessment in peer review differs fundamentally from general image quality evaluation: a figure must be visually legible, faithfully support the manuscript's claims, and communicate evidence with a clear visual hierarchy. However, if we apply traditional image assessment methods to scientific figure quality assessment, limitations emerge: classic IQA models capture perceptual quality or aesthetics but cannot judge whether a figure serves the paper's scientific argument; CLIP-based methods assess generic image-text correspondence, yet lack understanding of manuscript context; and zero-shot LLM/VLM judges, when repurposed for figure scoring, often yield overly concentrated scores with limited fusion of visual and textual evidence. We introduce an annotated dataset of 3,857 scientific figures from peer-reviewed conference papers, each rated along four peer-review-oriented dimensions: Clarity, Relevance, Informativeness, and Structure. We propose SciFigAlign, a fine-tuned multimodal scorer that grounds figure quality assessment in manuscript evidence. Given a figure crop, caption, citing paragraphs, and light paper context, SciFigAlign fine-tunes CLIP and SciBERT end-to-end with per-modality cross-attention and CubeMLP fusion, jointly optimizing SmoothL1 regression with a within-paper ranking hinge loss. Under paper-level splits, SciFigAlign achieves a macro MAE of 0.3524 and a within-paper pairwise accuracy of 81.64% on the test set with n = 396, a 59% relative error reduction over the best LLM-as-judge baseline with MAE 0.864. Ablations confirm that manuscript-grounded inputs, citing-context denoising, and ranking supervision are all critical, showing that scientific figure assessment requires learned alignment between visual content and manuscript evidence rather than prompting alone, even with state-of-the-art VLMs.

</details>


### [106] [Visual Credit Audit for Multimodal Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27069)
*Feixiang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Lanbo Sun,Nan Wei,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出视觉信用审计（VCA）方法，将封闭式空间基准测试的成功分解为正确性、额外图像支持和关系一致响应三个成分。实验发现12.73-26.25%的正确决策未获得图像信用，图像置换使依赖信用正确性下降21.25-47.80个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有封闭式空间基准测试可能在没有图像充分支持的情况下奖励正确答案，需要一种方法区分模型决策是否真正基于视觉证据。

Method: 提出视觉信用审计（VCA），包含两个估计量：1）基准图像是否比纯文本和空白对照提供更多支持（无需训练和标签）；2）模型是否响应关系特定的视觉证据。使用依赖性信用正确性（D-CC）、图像置换、固定像素关系对比和3x3证据来源析因设计等方法。

Result: 在四个开源多模态大模型和两个空间基准上，12.73-26.25%的正确决策未获得图像信用。同一分割图像置换使D-CC下降21.25-47.80个百分点。在受控的正确但未信用决策中，关系反转响应达81.57-100.00%，32.11%改变答案。

Conclusion: VCA能够将基准成功分解为正确性、额外图像支持和关系一致响应三个成分，揭示了模型在空间基准测试中决策的视觉依据程度。

Abstract: Closed yes/no spatial benchmarks can reward a correct answer even when the image adds little support beyond no-image contexts. Under a fixed forced-choice interface, Visual Credit Audit (VCA) separates two estimands: whether the benchmark image gives the model's declared decision more support than text-only and blank controls, and whether the model responds to relation-specific visual evidence. The first audit is training- and label-free and does not require an answer flip. Applying labels yields dependence-credited correctness (D-CC); on correct items, it equals same-control gold-aligned positive gain, while prediction alignment extends the audit to errors. Across four open MLLMs and two spatial benchmarks, 12.73-26.25% of decisions are correct yet uncredited. Matched same-split image permutation reduces D-CC by 21.25-47.80 points, with every paired 95% interval above zero. Fixed-pixel relation contrasts and a 3x3 evidence-source factorial show why null controls cannot identify relation response. Among controlled correct-but-uncredited agreement decisions, response to relation reversal spans 81.57-100.00%, while 32.11% pooled change answer. Independently audited outcomes on 108 geometry-compatible edits provide a bounded natural-image correspondence check. VCA thereby decomposes benchmark success into correctness, additional image support, and relation-consistent response.

</details>


### [107] [SciFigQual-Bench: A Benchmark for Scientific Figure Quality Assessment with Full-Manuscript Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27084)
*Zihan Deng,Chuanzhi Xu,Huiqi Liang,Haoyang Li,Xiaozhen Zhong,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigQual-Bench，一个面向科学论文图像的全文本上下文基准，从清晰度、布局、标题匹配、上下文相关性和误导风险五个维度评估图像质量，并设计SFQ-Agent跨模态评估框架，在测试集上（GPT-5.6-Sol驱动）实现了0.418的平均绝对误差和93.4%的一致性率，优于直接评估和辅助VLM评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像质量评估方法主要面向自然照片或AI生成内容，无法直接应用于科学论文中的图像；已有的学术图表研究局限于视觉表层比较，未能验证标题匹配度、引用相关性和视觉误导性。

Method: 构建了SciFigQual-Bench数据集（6,308张图像，2020-2025年顶级计算机科学会议，经多位领域专家在五个维度独立评分并聚合为金标准标注）；设计了SFQ-Agent分阶段跨模态评估框架，通过收集和融合模态证据实现可审计的精细化评分。

Result: 在eval1200测试子集上，SFQ-Agent（F3）搭配GPT-5.6-Sol取得了最低的平均绝对误差（0.418）和最高的一致性率（93.4%），持续优于直接评估和辅助（Sidecar）VLM评估方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的全文本上下文基准和跨模态评估框架在科学论文图像质量评估任务上有效且优越，为自动化评估科学图像质量提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific images are the core elements of presenting experimental conclusions, elaborating system architecture, and supporting comparative arguments in scientific papers. However, existing image quality assessment (IQA) methods are predominantly designed for natural photographs or AI-generated content, which cannot be directly applied to scientific papers. The few existing studies on scholarly charts remain confined to visual-surface comparisons, failing to verify caption alignment, citation relevance, or visual misleadingness. To address this, we propose SciFigQual-Bench, a full-text contextual benchmark that evaluates scientific images across five dimensions (clarity, layout, caption fit, context relevance, and misleading risk). The data covers top computer-science conferences from 2020 to 2025; 6,308 images were independently scored by multiple domain experts in five dimensions and aggregated into gold-standard annotations. Unlike previous scientific figure benchmarks, our dataset binds each image to its caption, citing sentence, and manuscript context. To enable automated evaluation on this benchmark, we designed a staged cross-modal evaluation framework SFQ-Agent to achieve auditable and refined scoring through the collection and fusion of modal evidence. Multiple mainstream large models were evaluated on the test subset eval1200, and SFQ-Agent (F3) equipped with GPT-5.6-Sol achieved the lowest overall average absolute error (0.418) and the highest consistency rate (93.4%), consistently outperforming both direct evaluation and auxiliary (Sidecar) visual language model evaluation schemes.

</details>


### [108] [Step-Attention Refinement of DINOv3 Features for Efficient Anterior Eye Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27087)
*Philippe Baumstimler,Jean-Mathieu Gagnon,Sébastien Gagné,Mathieu Duchesneau,Clément Playout,Lama Séoud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于轻量级DINOv3 ViT-Small骨干网络和逐步注意力特征精炼模块的眼前节（AES）分割方法，在临床数据集上达到85.55% mIoU，并展现出对域迁移的最强鲁棒性，验证了解码器设计对有效适配基础模型至医学分割任务的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼前节分割在眼科生物特征识别和临床图像分析中至关重要，但现有方法受限于异质采集条件和有限的医学标注数据，导致鲁棒性和泛化性不足。基础模型（如DINOv3）虽具有强大的迁移能力，但如何高效地将其表征适配到密集预测任务仍具挑战。

Method: 基于蒸馏后的DINOv3 ViT-Small骨干网络，提出逐步注意力特征精炼模块（step-attention feature refinement module），在卷积解码前逐步适配多层级Transformer特征，以少量参数高效利用预训练特征。

Result: 在包含333张临床图像（8种采集协议、7个解剖类别）的私有数据集上，全微调达到85.55% mIoU，优于卷积和Transformer基线方法（包括其他DINOv3方法）；在4个未见过的公开AES分割数据集上展现出最强的域迁移鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法为临床环境下鲁棒的AES分割建立了强基线，并突显了解码器设计对于有效适配基础模型表征至医学分割任务的关键作用。

Abstract: Anterior eye segment (AES) segmentation is a key component of both ocular biometrics and emerging clinical image analysis applications. However, heterogeneous acquisition conditions and limited annotations in medical settings hinder the robustness and generalization of existing methods. Foundation models (FMs) such as DINOv3 offer strong transfer capabilities, but efficiently adapting their representations to dense prediction tasks remains challenging. In this study, we investigate robust AES segmentation in clinical settings, and propose a lightweight architecture built upon a distilled DINOv3 ViT-Small backbone. We introduce a step-attention feature refinement module that progressively adapts multi-level transformer representations before convolutional decoding, enabling efficient exploitation of pretrained features with few parameters. We evaluate the proposed approach on a private dataset of 333 clinically acquired AES images spanning eight ophthalmic acquisition protocols and annotated for seven anatomical classes. Compared with convolutional and transformer-based baselines, including DINOv3-based methods, our approach achieves the best overall performance, reaching 85.55\% mIoU when fully fine-tuned. It also demonstrates the strongest robustness to domain shift across four unseen public AES segmentation datasets. These results establish a strong baseline for robust AES segmentation in clinical settings and highlight the importance of decoder design for effectively adapting FMs representations to medical segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [109] [FreqForcing: Autoregressive Long Video Generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27110)
*Jiatong Li,Leo Liang,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqForcing，一种无需训练的视频生成长度扩展框架，通过频谱自锚定（SSA）机制解决自回归视频扩散模型在长时生成中的误差累积问题，将5秒片段模型扩展至2分钟生成，实现24倍外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长时自rollout中存在误差累积，表现为色彩漂移、运动停滞和视觉崩溃；从频域角度分析，误差累积体现为低频带的能量偏移，而现有的attention sink机制虽能部分缓解但无法完全解决该问题。

Method: 提出FreqForcing框架，核心是频谱自锚定（SSA），利用锚点注意力的低频分量维持长时间视觉稳定性，同时通过局部注意力的高频分量保留动态运动；该框架无需重新训练，可直接扩展预训练模型。

Result: FreqForcing将预训练于5秒片段的Self-Forcing模型扩展至2分钟生成，实现24倍外推；实验表明其在定性和定量上均优于现有无训练方法，并与代表性有训练方法相当。

Conclusion: 频域分析有效揭示了自回归视频扩散模型中误差累积的本质，FreqForcing通过频谱自锚定实现了高效且无需训练的长视频生成扩展，为视频生成的时间外推提供了新思路。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable real-time streaming video generation. However, errors introduced during self-rollout accumulate over long horizons, manifesting as color drift, motion stagnation, and eventual visual collapse. In this paper, we characterize this phenomenon from a frequency-domain perspective: error accumulation appears as a pronounced energy drift in the low-frequency bands. We further investigate the effectiveness of attention sink in the frequency domain, and find that it improves the video quality by alleviating the spectral energy drift to some extent, but cannot fully resolve it. Motivated by the above analysis, we propose FreqForcing, a training-free framework that addresses error accumulation in long-video generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring (SSA). The proposed SSA leverages the low-frequency components of anchor attention to maintain long-horizon visual stability, while preserving dynamic motion through the high-frequency components of local attention. Our FreqForcing extends Self-Forcing pretrained on 5s clips to two-minute generation, achieving 24x extrapolation. Extensive experiments show that FreqForcing outperforms existing training-free methods quantitatively and qualitatively while remaining competitive with representative training-based approaches.

</details>


### [110] [Forensic Reproducibility Audit of a Radiology Vision-Language Model Benchmark: From Intended Protocol to Released Artifact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25589)
*Mateusz Kozłowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对一项胸部X光视觉语言模型(VLM)试点研究进行了回顾性取证可重复性审计，发现多项关键错误：DICOM渲染极性反转、数据集拆分丢失、标签提取截断、提示绑定混淆等。修正后Cochran's Q从154.73变为182.29，45个McNemar比较中27个原始p<0.05，20个经Holm校正后仍显著。作者撤回原始性能、排名、提示效应及临床声明，并提出了机器可验证的控制规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像AI基准测试通常假设各组件（数据集、渲染、提示、API、标签、代码、手稿等）之间的一致性，但缺乏实际验证。本文旨在通过回顾性审计揭示这种未经验证的一致性假设可能导致的严重问题。

Method: 对已保存的胸部X光视觉语言模型(VLM)试点研究进行回顾性取证可重复性审计，不重新调用模型或标注数据。追踪提示绑定、DICOM元数据、输出完整性、标签提取、匹配分析和发布传播等环节。

Result: 300次计划调用中297次返回非空报告；60次Claude调用中A/B标签使用了相同C提示；4张MONOCHROME1图像未进行所需极性反转；数据集拆分未保留；未验证的提取器将5份报告截断至4000字符；重建共同队列后Cochran's Q从154.73变为182.29；45个McNemar比较中27个原始p<0.05，20个经Holm校正后仍显著。

Conclusion: 原论文的性能、排名、提示效应和临床声明均被撤回。必须引入机器可验证的控制机制来确保未来医学影像AI基准测试中队列、DICOM渲染、提示和模型身份、调用状态、标注来源、关键分析和衍生制品的一致性。

Abstract: Medical-imaging AI benchmarks combine datasets, DICOM rendering, prompts, provider APIs, automated labels, statistical code, manuscripts, and repository releases. Agreement across these artifacts is usually assumed rather than tested. We performed a retrospective forensic reproducibility audit of a preserved chest-radiograph vision-language model (VLM) pilot; no model was called again and no image or report was newly annotated. We traced prompt bindings, DICOM metadata, output completeness, label extraction, matched analyses, and release propagation. Of 300 planned model-prompt calls, 297 yielded nonempty reports. Sixty Claude calls labeled A/B were executed with the same C prompt. The 30 studies represented 28 patients. Four MONOCHROME1 images were rendered without required polarity inversion, dataset split membership was not retained, and the unvalidated extractor truncated five reports to 4000 characters. Reconstructing one common cohort of 369 complete case-finding blocks changed Cochran's Q from 154.73 to 182.29. Of 45 McNemar comparisons, 27 had unadjusted p < 0.05 and 20 remained below 0.05 after Holm adjustment. These values describe only the archived automated-label matrix; they do not recover the intended prompt comparison or establish clinical performance. We withdraw the original performance, ranking, prompt-effect, and clinical claims and specify machine-verifiable controls for cohort, DICOM rendering, prompt and model identity, call status, annotation provenance, keyed analysis, and derived artifacts.

</details>


### [111] [Veritas++: Value-aware On-Policy Distillation for Perception-Enhanced AIGI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27113)
*Hao Tan,Jun Lan,Zichang Tan,Ajian Liu,Zijian Yu,Chuanbiao Song,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jun Wan,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Veritas++，一种感知增强的推理框架，通过面向感知的学习（PoRL）和值感知在线策略蒸馏（VaOPD）来弥补多模态大语言模型在AI生成图像检测中的感知瓶颈，实现可靠的泛化检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的AIGI检测器在捕捉细粒度异常方面存在感知瓶颈，它们主要关注视觉证据的组织与合成，而内在感知能力未得到充分优化。

Method: 提出Veritas++框架：(1) 将AIGI检测建立在三种基本感知能力上（细粒度视觉细节、语义异常、像素级差异）；(2) 引入面向感知的学习（PoRL），用可验证奖励替代开放式描述监督来强化感知能力；(3) 提出值感知在线策略蒸馏（VaOPD），通过自适应蒸馏机制优先传递高价值信号，利用特权自教师内化感知感知推理。

Result: 在标准、野外和新兴基准测试上的大量实验表明Veritas++实现了良好的泛化性能。感知学习有效弥合了感知差距，带来检测性能的无缝提升，VaOPD在保持现有性能的同时进一步实现高效能力进化。

Conclusion: Veritas++通过增强感知能力作为真实性推理的基础，有效解决了MLLM在AIGI检测中的感知瓶颈，实现了可靠的泛化检测，代码已开源。

Abstract: The growing capability of image generation models has made synthetic images a routine presence in open media, making robust and generalizable AI-Generated Image (AIGI) detection increasingly essential. While multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) offer a transparent alternative to black-box binary scoring, we observe that current MLLM-based detectors still exhibit notable perception bottlenecks in capturing fine-grained anomalies. They primarily focus on how visual evidence is organized and synthesized, leaving the intrinsic perception less optimized. To mitigate this gap, we present Veritas++, a perception-enhanced reasoning framework that establishes reliable perception as the foundation of authenticity reasoning. Rather than directly optimizing the model's explanatory ability, we ground AIGI detection on three basic perception abilities, i.e., capturing fine-grained visual details, semantic anomalies and pixel-level differences. Building on this insight, we introduce Perception-oriented Learning (PoRL), which replaces open-ended description supervision with verifiable rewards to explicitly strengthen these capacities. To further integrate enhanced perception with reasoning, we introduce Value-aware On-Policy Distillation (VaOPD), an adaptive distillation mechanism that prioritizes high-value distillation signals over uniform supervision, internalizing perception-aware reasoning through a privileged self-teacher. Extensive experiments across standard, in-the-wild and emerging benchmarks demonstrate that Veritas++ achieves promising generalization. The perception learning effectively bridges the perception gap and yields seamless gains on detection, while VaOPD further enables efficient capability evolvement without sacrificing existing performance. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/EricTan7/VeritasPP.

</details>


### [112] [Towards Grounded GI Endoscopy VQA via Multi-Task Learning on Small VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27122)
*Itbaan Safwan,Ramail Khan,Muhammad Annas Shaikh,Muhammad Atif Tahir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务微调方法，通过构建辅助的定位和描述任务，在无需额外标注的情况下提升VLM在胃肠道内镜VQA任务上的答案准确性及隐式视觉对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床采用胃肠道内镜VQA不仅要求模型能准确回答自由形式问题，还要求其内部表示能反映答案背后的视觉证据，但现有VLM在这类对齐能力上不足。

Method: 从现有VQA数据集中构建辅助定位和描述任务：复用专家标注的息肉掩码，对无真实掩码的类别利用GI域预训练分类器+Grad-CAM弱监督生成定位信号。采用低秩适配对三个小规模VLM骨干分别在纯VQA和多任务设置下进行微调。

Result: 多任务微调在分布内和分布外数据上均一致提升了答案准确性，同时改善了答案token与相关图像区域之间的隐式对齐。

Conclusion: 该多任务微调配方简单有效，能在不增加标注成本的前提下同时提升VLM的问答性能和视觉可解释性，有利于临床采纳。

Abstract: Gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopic image analysis has shifted from single-label classification toward visual question answering (VQA), where a model must answer free-form clinical questions about an image. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) achieve promising answer accuracy on this task, clinical adoption also requires the model's internal representations to reflect the visual evidence behind its answers. We propose a simple multi-task fine-tuning recipe that constructs auxiliary grounding and description tasks from an existing VQA dataset with minimal additional annotation: expert-annotated polyp masks are reused directly, while a GI-domain pretrained classifier with Grad-CAM localization provides weak supervision for finding categories that lack ground-truth masks. Three small VLM backbones are fine-tuned with low-rank adaptation under matched VQA-only and multi-task recipes on Kvasir-VQA-x1, and we show consistent accuracy gains together with improved implicit alignment between answer tokens and the relevant image region, evaluated on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data.

</details>


### [113] [SeasonStereo: Robust Dense Stereo Matching for Multi-Date Satellite Imagery via Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27139)
*Álvaro Díaz-Laureano,Roger Marí,Elías Masquil,Pablo Arias,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeasonStereo是一个可扩展的框架，通过合成图像对训练和基础模型的零样本几何先验，实现了从异时卫星图像进行视差估计，无需对齐的真实多时相训练数据或LiDAR标签，即可达到与最先进LiDAR监督模型相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星影像的精确3D重建通常依赖近同时立体像对，限制了其在多时相场景中的应用。多时相图像存在季节性变化和光照条件差异，而获得对齐的多时相图像和真实几何数据成本高昂，因此亟需一种鲁棒且可扩展的方法来应对这些外观变化。

Method: 提出SeasonStereo框架，通过合成图像对进行训练，这些图像对具有受控的季节性外观变化；同时利用基础模型提供的零样本几何先验来增强鲁棒性，从而无需真实对齐的多时相训练产品或LiDAR标签。

Result: SeasonStereo在精度上与最先进的LiDAR监督模型相当，同时能够生成更锐利的几何细节，且无需依赖对齐的真实多时相训练数据或LiDAR生成的标签。

Conclusion: SeasonStereo提供了一条降低监督成本、利用异质卫星图像进行大规模3D重建的实用路径。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction from satellite imagery typically relies on near-simultaneous stereo pairs, limiting its applicability to diachronic settings where multi-date images exhibit varying seasonal and illumination conditions. Training dense stereo matching models robust to appearance changes is a long-standing challenge, as aligned multi-date imagery and ground-truth geometry are costly to obtain at scale. We propose SeasonStereo, a scalable framework that addresses disparity estimation from diachronic satellite images by training on synthetic image pairs with controlled seasonal appearance variation, while leveraging zero-shot geometric priors from foundation models. SeasonStereo matches the accuracy of state-of-the-art LiDAR-supervised models, while producing sharper geometric details without requiring aligned real multi-date training products or LiDAR-derived labels. As a result, SeasonStereo offers a practical path toward large-scale 3D reconstruction from heterogeneous satellite images with reduced supervision cost.

</details>


### [114] [Explainable and Resource-Efficient Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs for Decision-Critical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27145)
*Piyush Jain,Kousik Dasgupta,Rajarshi Roy,Subarna Tripathi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ByDeWay-V2通过集成显式空间关系谓词（如“左侧”、“内部”）与深度线索，无需训练即可提升多模态大模型的空间推理能力，在资源受限场景下实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在决策关键型管道（如机器人、具身AI）中部署时，其空间判断的不透明性限制了操作者信任和可审计性；现有方法（如LDP）的粗粒度深度分层无法解决同一几何平面内的对象间空间关系（如投射关系和拓扑关系）。

Method: 利用开放词汇目标检测器（YOLO-World-L）计算检测对象间的成对几何关系，将结构化的空间谓词（如“left of”、“inside”）注入MLLM提示词中，桥接3D场景深度与2D空间语义，无需任何训练。

Result: 在BLINK空间子集上，ByDeWay-V2相较LDP在Qwen2.5-VL上实现了46%的相对F1提升；在VSR上，将BLIP-Base从接近随机水平恢复至0.53的F1值；最轻配置可在CPU上以严格40 token上下文预算运行。

Conclusion: ByDeWay-V2通过可读的空间关系谓词提供可审计证据，在无训练条件下有效缓解MLLMs的空间幻觉问题，并适用于资源受限的实时决策支持场景。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in decision-critical pipelines such as robotics, embodied AI, and safety monitoring, the opacity of their spatial judgments limits operator trust and auditability. MLLMs demonstrate strong reasoning but often struggle with fine-grained spatial understanding and object hallucination. Prior work, ByDeWay, introduced Layered-Depth-Based Prompting (LDP), a training-free framework that mitigates hallucinations by structuring prompts using monocular depth estimation. However, coarse depth layering falls short in resolving object-to-object spatial relationships within the same geometric plane, such as projective ("left of", "above") and topological ("inside", "touching") relations. We propose ByDeWay-V2, which integrates explicit spatial relational context alongside depth cues, expressed as human-readable predicates that serve as auditable evidence for downstream decision support. Using an open-vocabulary object detector (YOLO-World-L), our framework computes pairwise geometric relations between detected objects and injects them as structured spatial predicates into the MLLM prompt, bridging 3D scene depth and 2D spatial semantics without any training. We evaluate ByDeWay-V2 on the Visual Spatial Reasoning (VSR) and BLINK benchmarks across multiple MLLMs, with hallucination grounding assessed via POPE. On the BLINK spatial subset, ByDeWay-V2 achieves a 46 percent relative F1 improvement over LDP for Qwen2.5-VL, and recovers BLIP-Base's spatial reasoning on VSR from near-random performance to a competitive F1 of 0.53. Our lightest configuration operates under a strict 40-token context budget on CPU, showing the framework's suitability for resource-constrained, real-time decision-support settings.

</details>


### [115] [Anatomy Contextualized Adaption of CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27154)
*Roshan Kenia,Stephanie L McNamara,William Lotter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACA是一个轻量级框架，通过解冻的CT基础模型进行解剖级视觉-语言对齐，使用TotalSegmentator分解CT体素为解剖级嵌入，再经跨解剖Transformer精炼并与解剖级和扫描级文本对齐，在零样本分类任务中优于现有方法，训练时间不足1小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CT视觉-语言基础模型要么使用全容积表示稀释了细粒度解剖信号，要么在细粒度预训练中丢弃了全局上下文且从头训练计算成本高。ACA旨在同时实现解剖级对齐与全局上下文化的轻量级适配。

Method: ACA首先使用TotalSegmentator将CT体素分解为解剖级嵌入，然后通过跨解剖Transformer捕捉解剖间关系以增强全局上下文化，最后将这些嵌入与从放射报告中提取的逐解剖和扫描级文本进行对齐。

Result: 在Merlin和CT-RATE数据集上，ACA在零样本发现分类任务中一致优于冻结的基础模型基线和现有细粒度方法，且嵌入缓存后训练时间不足1小时。跨解剖Transformer的注意力权重显示出了合理的跨解剖上下文路由。

Conclusion: ACA是一种轻量级的适配方法，能够在保留并增强全局解剖上下文的同时，实现CT基础模型向解剖级视觉-语言对齐的迁移，具有高效和有效的特点。

Abstract: CT vision-language foundation models have demonstrated promising performance across downstream tasks, but are typically trained with whole-volume representations that dilute fine-grained anatomical signals. Fine-grained vision-language pre-training addresses this by aligning anatomy-level visual features with anatomy-specific text, but in doing so discards the global context that whole-volume models provide. Furthermore, existing fine-grained approaches train from scratch, making them computationally expensive. We introduce Anatomy Contextualized Adaptation (ACA), a lightweight framework that adapts frozen CT foundation model representations for anatomy-level vision-language alignment while enhancing global contextualization. ACA uses TotalSegmentator to decompose CT volumes into anatomy-level embeddings, which are refined via a transformer that captures cross-anatomy relationships, and aligned to both per-anatomy and scan-level text extracted from radiology reports. Evaluated on Merlin and CT-RATE, ACA consistently outperforms both the frozen foundation model baselines and existing fine-grained methods in zero-shot finding classification, while requiring less than one hour of training once embeddings are cached. The attention weights learned by ACA's inter-anatomy transformer additionally indicate plausible cross-anatomy context routing. Altogether, these results support ACA as a lightweight approach for adapting CT foundation models to anatomically grounded vision-language alignment while preserving and enhancing global anatomical context.

</details>


### [116] [HumanCLAW: Can Vision-Language Models Act Through a Body?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27180)
*Siyao Li,Jiawei Gu,Shuai Liu,Kairui Hu,Zekun Li,Linjie Li,Chengcheng Tang,Po-Chen Wu,Ivan Shugurov,Lingni Ma,Michael Zollhoefer,Sizhe An,Abhay Mittal,Amy Zhao,Ranjay Krishna,Manling Li,Ziwei Liu,Chuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanCLAW评估框架，将VLM的高层决策与底层运动控制解耦，通过原子技能命令和物理仿真评估VLM的动作智能。基于该框架构建的HumanCLAW-Bench包含1,218个长程导航-交互任务，测试9个最先进VLM，最高成功率仅16.8%。研究表明当前VLM缺乏具身自我意识，无法追踪自身身体状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在物理身体控制中的评估存在耦合问题——任务失败时难以区分是VLM决策错误还是运动控制器执行失败。需要一种解耦的评估方法，单独测量VLM的动作决策能力。

Method: 提出HumanCLAW框架：在每个时间步，VLM发出原子技能命令（如“伸手”、“行走”），命令被转换为亚秒级的连续全身运动，在真实物理仿真（含重力和碰撞）中执行。通过固定低层控制器来消除执行干扰，使VLM的动作智能成为唯一可测量变量。基于此构建HumanCLAW-Bench，包含41个室内场景的1,218个第一人称长程导航-交互任务。

Result: 测试9个最先进VLM，最高成功率仅16.8%。识别目标并非瓶颈，模型失败的主因是缺乏具身自我意识——无法追踪自身位置、是否到达目标、是否碰到障碍物。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在具身任务中的主要缺陷不是视觉识别能力，而是缺乏对自身身体状态的持续感知和跟踪能力，即具身自我意识。

Abstract: Evaluating whether a vision-language model (VLM) can act through a physical body is challenging. The outcome of an action couples the VLM's decision with motor control. When a task fails, it is hard to tell whether the VLM made a bad choice or the motor controller simply failed to execute it, e.g., losing balance and falling. In this work, we introduce HumanCLAW, an evaluation framework that decouples action decision-making from low-level execution. At every step, a harnessed, off-the-shelf VLM issues an atomic skill command, and the command is translated into a sub-second chunk of continuous full-body motion with real physical consequences, including gravity and collisions. The body can therefore act freely in the physical world, while execution-side disturbances, balance and motor errors, are factored out. What remains measurable is the model's action intelligence: its moment-to-moment choice of what the body should execute next. Based on this framework, we build HumanCLAW-Bench: 1,218 long-horizon, egocentric find-navigate-interact episodes across 41 indoor scenes. We test nine state-of-the-art VLMs and find that none solves the benchmark; the best model reaches only a 16.8% success rate. Recognizing the target is not the bottleneck. What current VLMs lack is embodied self-awareness: they lose track of their own body, failing to tell where it is, whether it has reached the goal, or whether it has hit an obstacle.

</details>


### [117] [VidMap: Exploiting Temporal Structure for Video-Based Structure-from-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27194)
*Zador Pataki,Paul-Edouard Sarlin,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VidMap系统，融合SLAM的时序约束与SfM的全局优化能力，利用宽基线密集匹配、时序优先闭环和单目深度先验，实现对任意未标定长视频的度量重建，在鲁棒性和精度上显著优于现有SLAM和SfM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM方法需已知相机标定、对初始化敏感且易发生瞬时失效，而SfM方法忽略图像顺序、对视觉对称性和极端运动缺乏鲁棒性，两者均无法鲁棒地从任意未标定长视频中准确恢复相机标定和度量位姿，限制了大规模导航与场景理解训练数据的获取。

Method: 设计VidMap系统：1) 利用宽基线密集图像匹配提取可靠对应关系；2) 将时序顺序作为一阶公民特征用于可靠的闭环检测；3) 在全局优化中引入度量单目深度先验，增强几何约束。融合SLAM的顺序强约束与离线SfM的灵活全局优化。

Result: 在涵盖极端运动和视觉对称性的多个挑战性数据集上，VidMap在鲁棒性和精度上均显著优于经典或学习的SOTA SLAM和SfM方法，无论是已知还是未知相机标定条件下。

Conclusion: VidMap有效弥合了SLAM与SfM之间的差距，能够对任意未标定长视频进行鲁棒的度量重建，为大规模导航和场景理解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurately recovering the camera's calibration and metric poses for any unconstrained video would unlock large-scale training data for navigation and scene understanding. The dominant approaches to this problem are severely limited: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) is sensitive to initialization and transient failures due to its causal, incremental nature; it is often over-optimized for real-time operation and generally requires known camera calibration; while Structure-from-Motion (SfM) typically forgoes any image ordering, enabling optimal initialization and global optimization, but lacks robustness to visual symmetries and extreme motions. To bridge this gap, we introduce a system that combines the strong sequential constraints of SLAM with the flexibility and global optimization of offline SfM, enabling the metric reconstruction of arbitrary, long, uncalibrated videos. This system leverages recent advances in wide-baseline dense image matching, treats temporal ordering as a first-class citizen for reliable loop closure, and augments global optimization with metric monocular depth priors. As a result, thorough evaluations on diverse, challenging datasets that exhibit extreme motion and visual symmetries reveal that our approach is significantly more robust and accurate than both state-of-the-art SLAM and SfM, classical or learned, with given or unknown camera calibration. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/cvg/vidmap.

</details>


### [118] [TurboVLA: Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Model at 32 Hz on an RTX 4090 with <1 GB VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27205)
*Hengyi Xie,Chenfei Yao,Xianjin Wu,Xuanyang Xi,Yiping Tang,Di Xu,Yingying Zhu,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurboVLA 提出了一种新的 VLA 范式，将传统的 V→L→A 路径（以 LLM 为中心）重构为直接的 V+L→A 映射，通过轻量级双向视觉-语言交互和紧凑解码器，在 LIBERO 上以仅 0.2B 参数、31.2ms 延迟和 0.9GB VRAM 达到 97.7% 的平均成功率，匹配或超越更大规模的 VLA 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的以 LLM 为中心的 VLA 模型（V→L→A）依赖大型语言模型作为感知与动作之间的中央接口，每次策略推理时都会产生巨大的计算和内存开销，效率低下。

Method: 提出 TurboVLA，将 V→L→A 路径重构为 V+L→A 直接映射：独立编码视觉观测和语言指令，通过轻量级双向视觉-语言交互进行信息交换，最后用紧凑解码器直接预测连续动作块，无需 LLM 作为中间桥梁。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上，TurboVLA（仅 0.2B 参数）实现了 97.7% 的平均成功率，推理延迟仅 31.2ms，推理 VRAM 仅 0.9GB（RTX 4090），匹配或超越了显著更大的 VLA 策略。

Conclusion: TurboVLA 证明了一种简洁高效的 VLA 新范式，为连接视觉、语言和动作以进行高效机器人操作提供了新视角，是现有 LLM 中心化 VLA 范式的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models commonly adopt an LLM-centric $V \to L \to A$ pathway, where visual observations are projected into the representation space of a large language model before being decoded into robot actions. Although effective, this design incurs substantial computation and memory overhead at every policy invocation. In this work, we introduce TurboVLA, a new VLA paradigm that reformulates the conventional $V \to L \to A$ pathway as a direct $V + L \to A$ mapping. Instead of using a large language model as the central interface between perception and action, TurboVLA independently encodes visual observations and language instructions, directly exchanges information between them through lightweight bidirectional vision-language interaction, and predicts continuous action chunks with a compact decoder. This simple design constructs task-conditioned representations directly from visual and linguistic features, significantly reducing the computational and memory costs of VLA inference. On LIBERO, TurboVLA achieves 97.7% average success with only 0.2B parameters, 31.2 ms inference latency, and 0.9 GB inference VRAM on a consumer-grade RTX 4090, matching or outperforming substantially larger VLA policies. These results establish TurboVLA as a simple and effective alternative to the prevailing LLM-centric VLA paradigm, offering a new perspective on how vision, language, and action can be connected for efficient robotic manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/TurboVLA.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Framework Implementation Maturity in Blockchain-Based Third-Party Compliance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26087)
*Jemima Owusu-Tweneboah,Amani Altarawneh,Deepti Gupta,Maria Luisa Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于许可区块链的第三方风险评估（TPRA）框架，旨在解决传统网络安全与隐私框架（如NIST SP 800-53、ISO/IEC 27001、GDPR、HIPAA）在实践中的不足——即通过时点审计、自我声明和碎片化证据审查等方式难以确保持续、独立且可验证的控制实施。该框架利用智能合约操作化评估工作流、执行多方治理并保留纵向评估状态，同时引入评估指标和定性成熟度模型，以衡量合规控制是否在多次评估周期中被可验证地实施、治理和维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全与隐私框架的采用依赖时点审计、自我声明和碎片化证据审查，无法提供控制措施持续实施、独立验证和长期维持的充分保证，尤其在涉及第三方供应商和多供应商生态系统（如医疗远程患者监测RPM）中问题更为突出。

Method: 提出基于许可区块链的TPRA框架，通过智能合约操作化评估工作流、执行多方治理并保留纵向评估状态；在此基础上设计评估指标和定性成熟度模型。

Result: 提出了一个区块链驱动的合规评估框架及其配套的成熟度评估方法，用于衡量合规控制的可验证实施、治理和持续维持情况。

Conclusion: 许可区块链技术可用于支持框架实施成熟度的度量，而非静态的合规验证，从而在多方参与环境中实现更可靠的持续合规评估。

Abstract: Cybersecurity and privacy frameworks such as NIST SP~800--53, ISO/IEC~27001, GDPR, and HIPAA are widely used to guide organizational security posture and regulatory compliance. In practice, however, framework adoption is often assessed through point-in-time audits, self-attestations, and fragmented evidence reviews, providing limited assurance that controls are consistently implemented, independently validated, and sustained over time, particularly in environments that rely on third-party vendors. These limitations are amplified in multi-vendor ecosystems, such as healthcare remote patient monitoring (RPM), where compliance obligations span organizational boundaries and assessments are conducted by multiple independent assessors. This paper investigates how permissioned blockchain systems can support framework implementation maturity measurement rather than static compliance verification. We propose a blockchain-based Third-Party Risk Assessment (TPRA) framework that operationalizes assessment workflows, enforces multi-party governance, and preserves longitudinal assessment state using programmable smart contracts. Building on this framework, we introduce a set of evaluation metrics and a qualitative maturity model designed to assess whether compliance controls are verifiably implemented, governed, and sustained across repeated assessment cycles.

</details>


### [120] [Lilith: Backdoor Generalization under Training-Inference Trigger Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26099)
*Zhou Feng,Jiahao Chen,Chunyi Zhou,Yuan Su,Tianyu Du,Yuwen Pu,Jianhai Chen,Jinbao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Lilith框架，研究后门攻击中训练时触发器向推理时触发器族泛化的问题，证明在触发器移位条件下后门仍能保持高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门研究仅评估精确触发器复用、训练时暴露的触发器多样性或沿预定义变换轴的变体，忽略了关键盲区：训练时学到的后门能否泛化到推理时未在训练中出现的触发器族。

Method: 提出Lilith黑盒锚点到触发器族框架：使用不相交的替代资源，首先用单一训练锚点诱导目标侧紧凑漏洞，然后构建有界的仅推理触发器族以保持锚点诱导的表征几何结构。通过锚点清除距离和族可达范围刻画机制，推导在局部正则性和有界替代-受害者差异下的族级目标保持充分条件。

Result: 跨数据集、架构、投毒率和防御的实验表明，Lilith实现了高族级攻击成功率、有限的效用下降和小的触发器泛化差距。附加分析表明族激活依赖于表征对齐而非提议机制。

Conclusion: 揭示了精确触发器评估所忽视的更广泛威胁，即后门攻击可以在训练-推理触发器移位条件下泛化，需要引起安全社区重视。

Abstract: Machine-learning services increasingly rely on public data, third-party providers, and outsourced training, creating opportunities for data-poisoning attacks that implant persistent malicious behavior while preserving benign utility. However, existing backdoor studies largely evaluate exact trigger reuse, training-exposed trigger diversity, or variations along predefined transformation axes. They therefore leave a critical blind spot: whether a backdoor learned from one training-time trigger can generalize to an inference-time trigger family absent from victim training. We formulate this problem as backdoor generalization under training--inference trigger shift and introduce Lilith, a black-box anchor-to-family framework. Using only disjoint surrogate resources, Lilith first induces a compact target-side vulnerability with a single training anchor, then constructs a bounded inference-only family that preserves the anchor-induced representation geometry. We characterize this mechanism through anchor clearance and family reach, deriving sufficient conditions for family-wise target preservation under local regularity and bounded surrogate--victim discrepancy. Experiments across datasets, architectures, poisoning rates, and defenses show that Lilith achieves high family-wise attack success with limited utility degradation and a small trigger generalization gap. Additional analyses show that family activation depends on representation alignment rather than the proposal mechanism, exposing a broader threat overlooked by exact-trigger evaluation.

</details>


### [121] [A Reference-Free Score for Detecting Silent Reasoning Failures in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26102)
*Vivek Shukla,Varun Shukla,Atul,Divya Mishra,Mehul Kumar Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出推理-答案忠实度评分(RAFS)，用于诊断数学链式推理(CoT)中推理过程与最终答案之间的不一致性，弥补了传统仅检查最终答案是否正确的评估缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数学CoT评估仅关注最终答案是否与参考答案匹配，导致两种错误：无效推理链可能偶然得到正确答案，而有效计算可能因转录错误被误判。这种"推理-答案一致性差距"无法区分正确结论与有效推导。

Method: RAFS是一种无参考、实例级别的诊断指标，包含四大组件：步骤有效性、推理到答案的蕴含关系与反事实敏感性、答案共识、条件推理稳定性。评估采用非补偿性聚合器，并形式化四种推理-答案结果类型，实例化语义轨迹距离，量化计算与弃权权衡，定义验证者独立性和功效分析。

Result: 在GSM8K和MATH上进行了预注册、结果盲的确认性研究，假设、可接受规则、校准和检验在确认性结果检查前固定。同时进行了可行性试点以验证端到端执行并估计干预覆盖率。

Conclusion: RAFS旨在通过可审计的预警信号，补充数学答案准确度评估，识别静默推理失败和答案提取错误，而不是评估模型的私有计算或数学事实的正确性。

Abstract: Mathematical chain of thought (CoT) evaluation is commonly reduced to whether the final answer matches a reference. This conflates producing a correct conclusion with producing a valid derivation an invalid chain can accidentally reach the right answer, while a valid calculation can be followed by a transcription error. We call this mismatch the reasoning answer consistency gap. This framework paper introduces the Reasoning Answer Faithfulness Score (RAFS), a reference free, instance level diagnostic of whether an emitted mathematical trace is locally credible, supports its answer, and is stable under resampling and targeted counterfactual interventions. RAFS combines step validity, reasoning to answer entailment and counterfactual sensitivity, answer consensus, and conditional reasoning stability. It evaluates transcript level agreement, not a models private computation and not factual correctness outside the tested mathematical setting. We retain a preregistered, results blind confirmatory study on GSM8K and MATH, with hypotheses, admissibility rules, calibration, and tests fixed before confirmatory outcomes are inspected. A separate feasibility pilot is specified to verify end to end execution and estimate interven tion coverage before that freeze numerical pilot claims are re ported only when trace level artifacts are available. We formalize four reasoning answer outcomes, justify the non compensatory aggregator, instantiate semantic trace distance, quantify compute and abstention tradeoffs, and define verifier independence and power analyses. RAFS is intended to complement mathematical answer accuracy with an auditable warning signal for silent reasoning failures and answer extraction errors

</details>


### [122] [GPT-Red: Automated Red Teaming via Self-Play at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26115)
*Eric Wallace,Christopher A. Choquette-Choo,Nikhil Kandpal,Sam Toyer,Dylan Hunn,Stephanie Lin,Yuxin Wen,Xiangyu Qi,Christopher Wolff,Zizhao Wang,Milad Nasr,Sicheng Zhu,Chuan Guo,Juan Felipe Cerón Uribe,Kaiwen Wang,Aiden Low,Kai Xiao,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GPT-Red，一个通过自对弈训练实现自动化红队测试的AI agent，用于发现针对前沿LLM的提示注入攻击，并通过对抗训练显著提升GPT-5.6的鲁棒性，其安全训练规模为目前公开最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM面临日益严重的提示注入攻击威胁，而人工红队测试成本高、扩展性差，需要一种自动化、可扩展的红队测试方法来持续评估和提升生产系统的鲁棒性。

Method: 设计可扩展的自对弈算法：GPT-Red攻击一个由同步训练的防御模型组成的多样种群，在逼真的红队环境中训练，使用了与最大RL后训练相当的算力资源。

Result: GPT-Red能够可靠突破GPT-5.5及之前的模型，发现比人工红队更多的成功攻击，并能泛化到未见过的环境、防御模型和工具链上。

Conclusion: 通过GPT-Red对抗训练出的GPT-5.6成为迄今最鲁棒的提示注入防御模型；随着防御模型不断增强，红队agent也能获得更好的学习信号，形成自我改进的飞轮效应。

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{GPT-Red}, an automated red-teaming agent that is trained to discover novel prompt injection attacks against frontier LLMs. The goal of this model is to evaluate and improve the robustness of our production systems. To this end, we use it to adversarially train GPT-5.6, our most robust model to prompt injections to date. To create GPT-Red, we design a scalable self-play algorithm where the model is tasked with attacking a diverse population of simultaneously-trained defender agents. We train the model on realistic red-teaming environments using compute on the same scale as some of our largest RL post-training runs, making it the single-largest LLM safety training run ever documented. GPT-Red excels at red-teaming: it reliably breaks our past models up to GPT-5.5, it finds more successful attacks than human red-teamers, and it generalizes to held-out environments, defender models, and harnesses. In the future, we expect that as we improve the robustness of each new GPT model, it will in turn will provide better learning signal for \textit{even stronger} red-teamer agents, thus unlocking a self-improvement flywheel.

</details>


### [123] [(EC)2: Event-Centric Explainability for Cybersecurity Through Multi-Agent LLM Investigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26201)
*Neta Kirmayer,David Tayouri,Andrés Murillo,Motoyoshi Sekiya,Asaf Shabtai,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出(EC)2框架，一种面向网络安全告警的事件中心、检测器无关的多智能体解释方法，能够通过结构化假设驱动调查生成基于可验证证据的操作性解释，改善告警后分析效率和事件分类准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测系统的特征级解释对安全运营中心的分析人员价值有限，分析师需要了解实体间的上下文关联和可操作性理解来有效处理告警。

Method: 提出(EC)2多智能体框架，采用事件中心、检测器无关的方法，通过结构化、假设驱动的调查流程生成基于可验证证据的告警解释。

Result: 该框架能够生成具有操作意义的解释，改善了告警后分析流程，并提升了事件分类准确率。

Conclusion: 事件中心的检测器无关解释方法能有效桥接异常检测输出与分析师实际需求之间的差距，为中小型企业网络安全运营提供更实用的告警分析支持。

Abstract: Security operations centers rely on anomaly detection systems to flag suspicious events. Feature-level explanations for anomaly detectors offer limited value for operational investigations. To effectively handle alerts, analysts need to know contextual relationships and need actionable understanding of the entities involved. This paper introduces an event-centric detector-agnostic approach for explaining cybersecurity alerts in small- to medium-sized enterprise networks. We present (EC)2, a multi-agent framework that performs structured, hypothesis-driven investigation to provide explanations grounded in verifiable evidence. Evaluation results show that the proposed framework improves post-detection analysis by generating operationally meaningful explanations, which also enhance event classification accuracy.

</details>


### [124] [On Exercising Governance Power in Decentralized Autonomous Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26204)
*Vabuk Pahari,Balakrishnan Chandrasekaran,Johnnatan Messias,Krishna P. Gummadi,Abhisek Dash*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了48个活跃的以太坊DAO的设计选择，揭示了治理机制中的信任与透明度权衡，并发现了一类新型攻击——治理攻击，该攻击即使在无漏洞实现中也能利用DAO治理合约的基本设计缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAO的治理合约设计对智能合约的安全性和隐私性有深远影响，但现有研究缺乏对设计选择中信任-透明度权衡的系统分析，以及对由此引入的关键漏洞的深入理解。

Method: 分析了48个活跃的以太坊DAO，将其治理设计选择分类为少数关键维度（如提案发起、投票机制、执行方式），并通过真实案例研究揭示设计缺陷。

Result: 发现了一类新型攻击——治理攻击，该攻击直接利用DAO治理机制的基本设计，即使合约实现无漏洞也无法避免。

Conclusion: DAO治理合约的设计选择存在内在的信任与透明度权衡，不当选择会引入严重漏洞；治理攻击是独立于实现正确性的根本性安全威胁。

Abstract: A decentralized autonomous organization (DAO) is a governance entity that allows its stakeholders to manage blockchain-based protocols through smart contracts. The DAO explicitly specifies how stakeholders make and enforce decisions concerning a protocol's operation in a smart contract, aptly referred to as its governance contract. The design of this governance contract, therefore, has far-reaching implications for the security (trust) and privacy (transparency) of the smart contracts managed by the DAO and its stakeholders. In this work, we (i) explicate the trust and transparency trade-offs of the design choices in implementing a DAO and (ii) highlight how poor choices introduce critical vulnerabilities, using real-world examples as case studies. To this end, we analyze $48$ public, actively used Ethereum-based DAOs that control a vast capital. We classify the design choices into a handful of key dimensions that succinctly capture how a DAO's stakeholders initiate a protocol change, vote on it, and, based on the voting outcome, execute that change. Our analyses crucially uncover a new class of attacks, which we call governance attacks, that directly exploit the fundamental design of a DAO's governance mechanisms, even if we assume bug-free implementations.

</details>


### [125] [Learning the Word Problem: Geodesic Lengths and Cryptographic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26241)
*Elisabeth Fink*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出WPNet，一种能启发式解决字问题的图神经网络架构，在Baumslag-Solitar群BS(1,2)和Artin群上展示效果，并成功应用于破解Wagner-Magyarik公钥密码系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字问题在后量子密码学中作为基础困难假设，但一些无限非阿贝尔群具有可解或算法高效的字问题，使其成为密码设计的吸引平台。本文旨在利用图神经网络启发式地解决字问题，并展示其对密码系统的安全威胁。

Method: 提出WPNet图神经网络架构，将未约化的词映射为动态图结构，模型在连续嵌入空间中学习对代数等价元素进行聚类，无需执行离散约化步骤即可识别词的测地线代表元。针对Wagner-Magyarik公钥密码系统部署模型变体以预测未约化词的测地线长度。

Result: WPNet在Baumslag-Solitar群BS(1,2)和Artin群上成功启发式解决字问题，能够准确预测未约化词的测地线长度，并成功攻破Wagner-Magyarik公钥密码系统。

Conclusion: 字问题的结构泄露具有密码学严重性，基于图神经网络的方法可以启发式解决原本困难的字问题，从而对依赖该困难假设的后量子密码系统构成实际威胁。

Abstract: The Word Problem has been a subject of intensive mathematical study for over a century, initially driving advances in combinatorial group theory and more recently emerging as a foundational hardness assumption in post-quantum cryptography (PQC). While generally undecidable, several families of infinite non-abelian groups exhibit solvable or algorithmically fast word problems, making them attractive platforms for cryptographic design. This paper introduces WPNet, a novel Graph Neural Network architecture capable of solving the Word Problem heuristically, which is demonstrated on the Baumslag-Solitar group $BS(1,2)$ and on an Artin group. By mapping unreduced words to dynamic graph structures, the model learns to cluster algebraically equivalent elements in a continuous embedding space, effectively identifying the geodesic representative of a word without executing discrete reduction steps. As an application, a model variant is developed that can predict the geodesic length of an unreduced word in both groups. To demonstrate the cryptographic severity of this structural leakage, WPNet is successfully deployed against the Wagner-Magyarik public-key cryptosystem.

</details>


### [126] [StealthBench: Measuring Operational Stealth in Autonomous Offensive-Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26314)
*Ads Dawson,Adrian Wood*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出StealthBench基准，用于评估自主攻防安全代理在六项OPSEC维度上的隐蔽性。测试发现所有模型的安全成功率均不超过54%，表明OPSEC失败是跨模型家族的系统性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精英安全研究者和高级持续性威胁(APT)能在不被察觉的情况下达成目标，但自主代理虽继承了同样的进攻任务，却未继承相应的隐秘操作技艺(tradecraft)，导致在安全操作中频繁暴露踪迹。

Method: 从真实漏洞赏金和红队渗透轨迹中提取11个经人工验证的OPSEC事件，扩展为14个Docker化任务场景；使用3模型LLM评审委员会(多数投票)评估代理轨迹，衡量安全成功率、Stealth@Solve和鲁莽解决率。

Result: 没有模型的安全成功率超过54%，表明OPSEC失败在所有模型家族中具有系统性。

Conclusion: 当前自主安全代理在隐蔽性方面存在系统性缺陷，StealthBench可作为开发隐蔽感知代理和自动化OPSEC监控的公共基准。

Abstract: Stealth, the discipline of achieving an objective without revealing your presence, capabilities, or collected intelligence, is what separates sophisticated operators from detectable ones. Elite security researchers and advanced persistent threats achieve their objectives unnoticed; autonomous agents increasingly inherit the same offensive tasks, but do they inherit the tradecraft? We introduce StealthBench,a benchmark that measures operational stealth in autonomous offensive-security agents across six operational security (OPSEC) dimensions. We extract 11 hand-verified OPSEC incidents from real bug-bounty and red-team trajectories, expanded into 14 dockerized task scenarios, where agents, despite finding real vulnerabilities, committed stealth failures inconsistent with standard operational tradecraft: embedding credentials in public uploads, deleting production resources to prove access, force-adding uninvolved users to demonstrate a race condition.
  We evaluate agent trajectories using a 3-model large language model (LLM) judge panel with majority-vote aggregation, measuring safe success rate (solved and stealthy), Stealth@Solve (tradecraft quality among successful solves), and reckless solve rate (solved but cover blown). Our results show that no model exceeds 54% safe success rate (the compound metric requiring both task completion and stealth), confirming that OPSEC failures are systematic across model families. We release StealthBench as a public benchmark to support both the development of stealth-aware agents and automated OPSEC monitoring for autonomous offensive-security deployments. The interactive leaderboard, evaluation harness, and dataset are available at https://stealthbench.com.

</details>


### [127] [A Controlled Candidate-Set Benchmark for Offline Satellite-Security Plan Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26371)
*João Paolo Cavalcante Martins Oliveira,Lucas Teske,Paulo Matias*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低秩分解适配器（low-rank decomposition adapter），并发布了一个卫星安全领域的案例分离语料库（24个 authored decompositions 和 83个 next-step examples）。在受控的SPARTA候选集上评估，结果表明适配器在格式合规性上有优势，但整体性能不足以作为可靠的独立分解系统，仅作为概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 某些安全-发展场景需要能够将目标映射为有序、可检查计划的局部模型，现有方法在参考计划泄露控制和格式合规方面存在不足。

Method: 提出低秩分解适配器；构建案例分离的卫星安全语料库（24个场景）；采用SPARTA候选集（含8个打乱候选项）防止参考计划泄露；通过oracle包含所有参考技术构建评估集；进行多维度对比（不同适配器、Qwen2.5基线、解码种子、训练种子等）及留一法交叉验证。

Result: 1.5B参数下适配器精确率0.583（两样本提示0.480），召回率0.586（两样本提示0.660）；案例重采样区间在1.5B和7B时跨越零；适配器格式合规性显著更好；自回归next-step准确率在0.5B/1.5B/7B分别为0.08/0.06/0.29。

Conclusion: 本研究仅作为概念验证，不能证明适配器是可靠的独立分解系统或具有普遍优势；提供了公式化方法、泄露控制数据集、可复现包和描述性分析；需进行额外的诊断测试以检查合约复用问题。

Abstract: Some security-development settings require local models that map an objective to an ordered, checkable plan. We present a low-rank decomposition adapter and release a case-disjoint corpus with 24 authored decompositions and 83 derived next-step examples across 24 satellite-security cases. To prevent reference-plan leakage, prompts contain one objective and eight shuffled SPARTA candidates. The evaluation candidate sets are constructed with oracle inclusion of every reference technique, so this study measures selection from a controlled set, not retrieval. On six fixed cases and five decoding seeds, mean scores vary across adapters and prompted Qwen2.5 baselines; a separate greedy check over three adapter training seeds shows non-negligible training variance. A 24-fold greedy leave-one-case-out control at the two compact sizes likewise shows tradeoffs: at 1.5B, adapter precision is 0.583 against 0.480 for two-shot prompting, while recall is 0.586 against 0.660. Case-resampling intervals for adapter precision relative to the strongest prompted baseline cross zero at 1.5B and 7B. The adapter is also substantially more format-compliant, which prevents attributing all score differences to selection. Autoregressive next-step accuracy is 0.08, 0.06, and 0.29 at 0.5B, 1.5B, and 7B. We therefore offer the formulation, leakage-controlled dataset, reproducibility package, and descriptive analysis as a proof of concept, not evidence of a reliable stand-alone decomposition system or a general adapter advantage. A separate diagnostic tests contract reuse.

</details>


### [128] [QUIC-TRIP: A Triple-Redundant Journey Toward Secure Substation Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26379)
*Jorge David de Hoz Diego,Ioannis Zografopoulos,Anca Jurcut*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QUIC-TRIP，一种基于传输层（L4）的透明安全方法，用于保护R-GOOSE等工业通信协议。该方法通过封装数据流、支持多路径冗余传输和低延迟代理，在Frankfurt-Amsterdam链路上实现比OpenVPN更低的RTT，仅比DTLS 1.2高2.88%，同时提供三重冗余防御机制，通信开销为32.18%/路径，有效平衡了电网关键通信的韧性与带宽成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电力系统依赖R-GOOSE等实时变电站通信协议，但这些协议缺乏内置安全机制，优先保障可用性而忽视机密性和完整性，易受虚假数据注入和DoS攻击。随着通信扩展到广域网和公共网络，这一脆弱性加剧，亟需符合DOE纵深防御和零信任指南的安全方案。

Method: 提出QUIC-TRIP框架，在OSI传输层（Layer 4）透明代理数据流，无需修改现有协议端点。利用QUIC协议的低延迟特性和多路径能力，实现三重冗余传输：通过三条不同路径同时发送副本，转发最早到达的副本并丢弃后续重复包，提供DoS洪水攻击下的韧性。

Result: 在Frankfurt-Amsterdam链路上，QUIC-TRIP底层传输代理的平均RTT低于OpenVPN，仅比集成DTLS 1.2（含会话复用）高2.88%。在DoS洪水攻击下，QUIC-TRIP通过多路径冗余通信成功保障R-GOOSE消息的可靠传输，每条启用代理路径的通信开销为32.18%。

Conclusion: QUIC-TRIP为时间敏感的电网通信提供了有效的安全增强方案，在不影响现有协议运行的前提下实现了低延迟、高韧性的透明安全防护，在韧性与带宽成本之间提供了可控的权衡，满足现代电力系统的安全合规要求。

Abstract: Modern power systems rely on real-time substation communication protocols, such as the Routable Generic Object-Oriented Substation Event (R-GOOSE), for critical control and protection functions. However, these protocols often lack built-in security features and prioritize availability over confidentiality and integrity, making them susceptible to false data injection and denial-of-service attacks. This vulnerability is exacerbated when communications are transmitted over wide-area or public networks. Addressing these cyber threats is essential to comply with current security mandates, including the DOE's defense-in-depth and zero-trust guidelines. This paper introduces QUIC-TRIP, a transparent security methodology for low-latency IP-based industrial communications. By operating at the Open Systems Interconnection (OSI) Transport Layer (Layer 4), the solution encapsulates and protects data flows without affecting the operation of existing protocol endpoints. Baseline echo Round-Trip Time (RTT) results over a Frankfurt-Amsterdam communication path show that the underlying transport-layer proxy used by QUIC-TRIP achieves a lower average RTT than OpenVPN and only 2.88% higher average RTT than integrated DTLS 1.2, even with DTLS session reuse. We evaluate the resilience of QUIC-TRIP multipath communication under DoS flooding by securing R-GOOSE communications. In these tests, traffic is transparently delivered through three different paths, and QUIC-TRIP forwards the earliest-arriving duplicate while discarding later copies. The framework provides a triple-redundant defense scheme with a measured communication overhead of 32.18% per enabled proxied path, offering a bounded trade-off between resilience and bandwidth cost for time-critical grid operations.

</details>


### [129] [Recover, Decode, Reguard: Guard-Agnostic Defense Amplification againstEncoded VLM Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26574)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zhuoxi Wang,Shibo Zheng,Zijian Xiao,Xiangchen Guan,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言模型的安全分类器存在"解码鸿沟"：攻击者通过编码转换（如集合论、稀有语言、代码、图像文本等）可绕过检测。本文提出一种与守卫无关的"恢复-解码放大器"，将编码内容还原为明文后送入守卫，但仍无法同时实现低攻击成功率和低过度拒绝。最佳守卫+放大器组合仍使63-65%的有害行为成功，而添加重守卫层虽能降低攻击率，却导致81-92%的良性过度拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型的安全分类器仅检查输入的表面形式而非语义含义，攻击者通过编码转换（如形式逻辑、稀有语言、代码、图像文本等）即可绕过检测。作者希望设计一种与具体守卫无关的预处理方法，弥合这一"解码鸿沟"。

Method: 提出一个guard-agnostic的Recover-and-Decode放大器，在守卫之前将图像内容转录、将编码文本还原为明文。采用best-of-suite评估（类似AutoAttack），对11种攻击进行集成测试，在5种守卫和2种目标VLM上系统评估。

Result: 未加防御的集成攻击可攻破89-91%的有害行为；最佳守卫+放大器仍留下63-65%；放大器仅在10个守卫-目标对中的4个有显著提升。添加重守卫层可将攻击率大幅降低，但导致良性过度拒绝率飙升至81-92%。没有任何配置能同时达到低攻击成功率和低过度拒绝率。

Conclusion: 对于representation-shifting攻击（编码和跨模态渲染），当前基于恢复的VLM防御方法存在根本性的安全-效用天花板，无法兼顾安全性与可用性。论文贡献了放大器方法、集成评估方法以及该防御范式有效与失效的边界地图。

Abstract: Safety classifiers ("guards") are the dominant black-box defense for vision-language models, yet they judge an input's surface form, not its meaning: a harmful request re-encoded as set theory, formal logic, a rare language, code, or an image of text slips past a guard that would block it in plain language -- the decode gap. The natural fix is a guard-agnostic recover-and-decode amplifier that transcribes image content and restates encoded text into its plain payload before the guard, so any off-the-shelf classifier can screen the true request. We build this amplifier and evaluate it against the attacker's best case: an ensemble of eleven attacks, scoring a behavior as broken if any succeeds (best-of-suite, following AutoAttack) -- rarely reported for jailbreak defenses, yet ~3.5x the per-attack mean. This exposes our central finding: an empirical safety-utility ceiling for the non-iterative recovery defenses we evaluate, across five guards and two target VLMs. The amplifier only partly closes the gap -- the undefended ensemble breaks 89-91% of behaviors, and the best guard-plus-amplifier still leaves 63-65% -- and its gain over the guard alone is significant in only four of ten guard-target pairs. It is guard-agnostic at the interface, but not uniformly so in effect. A modular reguard layer closes much of the residual, yet drives benign over-refusal to 81-92% for well-calibrated guards; the one laxer guard that stays usable never reaches deployable safety (48% ensemble ASR). No configuration we evaluate reaches both low attack-success and low over-refusal, for the pipeline we study and for representation-shifting attacks -- encodings and cross-modal renders that leave a legible payload, not pixel- or embedding-space attacks. We contribute the amplifier, an ensemble evaluation that makes the trade-off visible, and a map of where recovery-based VLM defense works and where it does not.

</details>


### [130] [CDN Tsunami: Exploiting HTTP/3-HTTP/1.1 Conversion for DoS Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26589)
*Ziyu Lin,Tianlong Su,Yingjie Lin,Prosanta Gope,Yinzhi Cao,Ximeng Liu,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了HTTP/3在CDN部署中引发的DoS攻击，利用CDN与源站间HTTP/3和HTTP/1.1协议异构部署的差异，设计了两种放大攻击（HBA和HCA），发现42,330个潜在易受攻击的子域名，并已获得两家CDN厂商的确认与修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTTP/3在CDN中引入，但其与源站HTTP/1.1之间的异构部署可能带来新的DoS攻击面，而此前研究未涉及这一领域。

Method: 设计两种攻击变种：HTTP/3带宽放大攻击（HBA）和HTTP/3连接放大攻击（HCA），利用CDN采用HTTP/3而源站使用HTTP/1.1的协议差异，将少量HTTP/3流量放大为大量HTTP/1.1流量。基于Tranco Top 1M域名列表进行大规模测量评估。

Result: 识别出42,330个潜在易受攻击的子域名；两家CDN厂商已确认漏洞并给予奖励，部署了缓解措施。

Conclusion: HTTP/3与HTTP/1.1异构部署会引入新的DoS攻击风险，攻击者可通过协议差异实现流量放大，需在CDN部署中加以防范。

Abstract: Content Delivery Networks (CDNs) provide high availability, accelerate content delivery for their host websites, but are also vulnerable to different types of Denial-of-Service (DoS) attacks. Prior works have studied a variety of DoS attacks with HTTP/1.1 or HTTP/2 connections, but most of them are being fixed, making CDNs robust against such attacks. One unexplored research area is how the recent introduction of HTTP/3 at CDNs affects the DoS attack landscape, especially when there are heterogeneous deployments of HTTP/3 and HTTP/1.1 between CDNs and host websites.
  In this paper, we design the first study of DoS attacks against HTTP/3 protocols deployed at CDNs. Our key insight is that when the CDN adopts HTTP/3 but the host websites use HTTP/1.1, an adversary can utilize the disparity to amplify a small amount of traffic to the CDN using HTTP/3 to a large amount from the CDN to the host website using HTTP/1.1. More specifically, we design two attack variations-HTTP/3 Bandwidth Amplification (HBA) and HTTP/3 Connection Amplification (HCA)-targeting the bandwidth and the number of connections, respectively. Furthermore, we conduct a large-scale measurement upon the Tranco Top 1M domain list to quantify the real-world impact of these attacks, identifying 42,330 subdomains that are potentially vulnerable to our attacks. Finally, we responsibly disclose the details of our attacks to the affected CDN vendors: so far, two vendors have already acknowledged their vulnerabilities with bounties and have deployed our mitigations.

</details>


### [131] [Guarding Organizations Against Malware Risk: A Novel Graph-Based Malware Detection Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26634)
*Yinan Gao,Jiarong Xu,Xiaohang Zhao,Xiao Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MalGuard是一种基于图的恶意软件检测方法，通过操作角色识别和程序图表示学习两项创新，提升了恶意软件检测性能并降低了未检测恶意软件的预期成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织数字化扩大了网络安全风险，现有基于字节的机器学习方法易受逃避行为影响，而图方法虽能捕获执行行为但无法识别协同实现程序行为的基块群组，且未能学习到足够表达力的程序图表示。

Method: MalGuard提出两项方法创新：1) 操作角色识别方法，识别基块的协同群组作为操作角色，捕获孤立基块不可见的程序行为；2) 程序图表示学习方法，通过建模操作角色间交互、保留稀疏恶意信号、捕获层次图结构来学习表达性表示。

Result: 广泛实验表明，MalGuard提升了检测性能，并降低了未检测恶意软件的预期成本。

Conclusion: MalGuard通过图方法有效解决了恶意软件检测中的逃避行为问题，为组织恶意软件风险管理提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Organizational digitalization expands cybersecurity risks, making cybersecurity an increasingly important research area in Information Systems (IS). Among these risks, malware has become a pervasive and destructive threat. Byte-based machine learning (ML) methods are widely used for malware detection but remain vulnerable to evasive behaviors that manipulate raw bytes to evade detection. Graph-based methods are less affected by such manipulations because they represent software as program graphs that capture execution behavior. However, they do not explicitly identify cohesive groups of basic blocks that jointly realize meaningful program behaviors, nor do they learn sufficiently expressive program graph representations for accurate detection. To this end, we propose MalGuard, a graph-based malware detection method for organizational malware risk management. MalGuard introduces two methodological innovations: an operational role identification approach and a program graph representation learning method. The former identifies these cohesive groups of basic blocks as operational roles, enabling the detector to capture program behaviors that may not be visible from isolated basic blocks. The latter learns expressive program graph representations by modeling interactions among operational roles, preserving sparse malicious signals, and capturing hierarchical graph structure. Extensive experiments show that MalGuard improves detection performance and reduces the expected cost of undetected malware.

</details>


### [132] [Borrowed Strength: Best-of-N Search over a Code EncodingBreaks Self-Check Jailbreak Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26639)
*Haoyu Zhang,Shibo Zheng,Xiangchen Guan,Zhuoxi Wang,Zijian Xiao,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示SAGE自检防御（号称99%成功率）可被组合攻击破解：将代码补全编码与最佳N搜索组合，在三个开源目标上达到67%/22%/15%攻击成功率（70B模型同样有效）。分析表明，自检防御本质依赖目标模型自身判断（32%-97%概率转化为明确拒绝），不同防御类型（变换/门控）对攻击的抵抗方向完全相反，且攻击的成功取决于其向决策边界传递的独立探测次数。此外，报告了确定性攻击在贪心解码下无最佳N变化通道的缺陷及单行诊断方法。所有结论基于31万次人类验证的生成评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自检防御（如SAGE）声称接近完美的防御成功率（99%），但未充分检验其面对组合攻击时的鲁棒性。需要揭示这类防御的根本弱点及被突破的机制。

Method: 组合两种已知攻击：代码补全编码（code-completion encoding）和最佳N搜索（best-of-N search），分别单独对SAGE几乎无效（≤4.7%行为成功率），但组合后搜索预算用于编码，在三个开源目标上达到显著成功率。通过分析防御类型（变换防御vs门控防御）对攻击的差异响应，以及探测次数与决策边界的关系来解释组合攻击效果。

Result: 组合攻击在三个开源目标上分别达到67%/22%/15%成功率，70B模型上效果持续。自检防御本质弱点：SAGE依赖目标模型自身判断，32%-97%概率转化为明确拒绝。不同防御类型对攻击抵抗方向完全相反。发现并修复了确定性攻击在贪心解码下无最佳N变化通道的缺陷。

Conclusion: 自检防御的强度来源于目标模型自身，而非外部检测机制，因此可以被针对模型特性的组合攻击有效突破。防御与攻击之间存在可解释的对抗关系（探测次数与决策边界），而非简单的成功率数字对比。

Abstract: A self-check defense asks the target model to assess a request before answering it; SAGE, the strongest published instance, reports an average 99% defense success rate. We show it can be breached by composing two attacks that are individually harmless against it: an established code-completion encoding and an established best-of-N search, neither of which exceeds 4.7% of behaviors alone. Composed, with the search budget spent on the encoding, they reach 67/22/15% across three open targets, and the effect persists on a 70B target. We then explain the composition rather than only reporting it. First, a self-check defense borrows its strength from the target: SAGE does not detect the attack, it asks the model to, and the four targets convert that request into an explicit refusal between 32% and 97% of the time, which orders the spread in defended coverage even though undefended reach is near-identical. Second, which attack survives is decided by the type of defense, and it inverts: against transform defenses the code encoding retains far more of its undefended reach than the character search, while against gate defenses the ordering flips. We account for this with the number of independent probes an attack delivers to a defense's decision boundary. Finally, we report a validity defect we found and repaired in our own pipeline, a deterministic attack under greedy decoding has no best-of-N variation channel at all, and give the one-line diagnostic that detects it. All claims rest on 310,000 generations scored by a human-validated judge.

</details>


### [133] [Fingerprint-Driven Automation: Coupling Reconnaissance with POC Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26655)
*Hongping Wang,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一款高自动化、用户友好的网络侦察与漏洞验证工具，解决了现有工具准确性低、隐蔽性差、耗时长、集成度不合理等问题，通过在线安全仿真靶场测试验证了其在自动化侦察、深度数据处理及自动漏洞检测与验证方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全侦察工具存在信息收集准确率低、隐蔽性不足、耗时长、工具集成度不合理（过低或过高导致难以使用）以及功能可扩展性差等问题，严重影响用户的工作效率和体验，因此需要设计一款更友好、更自动化的侦察与漏洞验证工具。

Method: 提出一种高自动化侦察与漏洞验证工具的设计方案，包括功能设计与基本架构，随后对全部功能进行具体实现与测试，通过在在线安全仿真靶场中进行实验验证工具的可行性。

Result: 在在线安全仿真靶场上的测试结果表明，该工具能够完成对特定目标的自动侦察与深度数据处理，并实现自动化的漏洞检测与验证。

Conclusion: 该工具在自动化程度、检索便捷性及网络安全作战使用方面具有显著优势，能够有效提升侦察与漏洞验证的效率和用户体验。

Abstract: In the field of network security confrontation, reconnaissance is the first and most critical step. Accurate, efficient, and comprehensive reconnaissance can help network security workers more fully understand the target's current state, identify potential weaknesses, and formulate a targeted attack strategy. However, there are some problems in the existing tools on the market, such as low accuracy of the collected information, lack of concealment, time-consuming, low or high integration of tools, which makes them difficult to start using. With the continuous development of technology, systems need to continuously upgrade their technologies and strategies, and expand new functions. The scalability of tool functions is also one of the factors that need to be taken into consideration. These problems in the practice of network security have a serious impact on user experience and work efficiency. In view of this, a more user-friendly, more automated reconnaissance and vulnerability verification tool is designed.This paper proposes to develop a highly automated reconnaissance and vulnerability verification tool. This paper presents the tool functions and conducts the basic design. Finally, we implement and test all the functions specifically to verify the feasibility of the tool functions and highlight the advantages of this tool in terms of automation, retrieval, and use in network security operations.Through the test of the online security simulation range, the results show that this tool can complete the automatic reconnaissance and in-depth data processing of specific targets, and conduct automatic vulnerability detection and verification.

</details>


### [134] [Graph Is the Verifier: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Interprocedural Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26656)
*Yikun Li,Ting Zhang,Jiakun Liu,Jinfeng Jiang,Yuheng Yieh,Yixin Yang,Wen Bin Leow,Yide Yin,Yintong Huo,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出VulAgentRL框架，利用智能体强化学习和代码属性图实现跨函数漏洞检测，通过图节点ID精确验证证据，在多项指标上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的漏洞检测器大多孤立分类单个函数，但71.7%的真实漏洞函数需要跨函数证据才能正确分类；而智能体RL虽然能收集证据，但缺乏可靠奖励信号（基于最终判决的奖励可被不执行任何调查就获得）。

Method: 提出VulAgentRL框架：(1) 基于代码属性图(CPG)，推理时策略查询调用者、被调用者、数据流等信息；(2) 训练时同一CPG验证策略引用的证据，利用CPG节点持久整数ID实现精确比较而非文本匹配；(3) 通过蒸馏教师调查来初始化策略，作为必要预热。

Result: 在仓库级划分（防止泄露）下，VulAgentRL在严格的pair-wise-correct指标上优于包括前沿模型在内的SOTA基线，且工具调用次数更少；在分布外语料和类别不平衡场景下优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 基于CPG的智能体RL框架能够有效解决跨函数漏洞检测问题，精确的证据验证机制和蒸馏预热策略是关键组成部分。

Abstract: Real-world vulnerabilities often span multiple functions, yet most learning-based detectors classify each function in isolation: on a sample of real CVEs, we find that 71.7% of vulnerable functions require evidence from outside the function to be classified correctly. Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) could close this gap by enabling a model to gather that evidence itself, but it lacks a reliable reward, since a reward defined on the final verdict alone can be obtained without performing any investigation. We propose VulAgentRL, an agentic RL framework for interprocedural vulnerability detection built on a Code Property Graph (CPG). The CPG serves two roles: at inference time the policy queries it for callers, callees, dataflow, and other queries, and at training time the same graph verifies the evidence the policy cites. Because every CPG node carries a persistent integer identifier, this verification is an exact comparison rather than a textual match, so the reward credits verdicts that are supported by evidence. We further initialize the policy by distilling teacher investigations, and show that this warm start is necessary, since RL cannot acquire tool-use behavior it never samples. Under a repository-level split that prevents leakage, VulAgentRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, including frontier models, on the strict pair-wise-correct metric while issuing fewer tool calls, and its advantage persists on an out-of-distribution corpus and under class imbalance.

</details>


### [135] [Not In My Git Yard: Catching Backdoors at Commit and Release Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26719)
*Dimitri Kokkonis,Michaël Marcozzi,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Lily是一种自动化方法，通过将后门检测机制集成到CI流水线和发布审查流程中，利用增强型模糊测试和代码变更分析，有效检测和阻止开源软件中的代码级后门注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件面临代码级后门注入的持续威胁，现有CI流水线无法检测此类攻击，下游二进制分析工具需要大量人工干预，且已知的真实后门事件仅凭运气和人工审查才得以阻止。

Method: Lily通过两个关键贡献实现后门检测：(1) 增强CI兼容的模糊测试能力，基于历史和当前软件执行检测可疑行为的触发条件；(2) 将代码变更分析与模糊测试数据结合，精确定位揭示后门的代码区域。同时设计了五种对抗策略及相应防御措施。

Result: 在数百个良性及包含后门的提交和发布上的实验表明，Lily实现了高检测准确率和低误报率，能可靠识别恶意代码，抵抗对抗性攻击，并可预防真实世界中的后门事件。

Conclusion: Lily是一种有效的自动化后门检测方法，能够无缝集成到现有开发流程中，填补了当前CI流水线在安全检测方面的空白，为开源软件供应链安全提供了重要保障。

Abstract: Code-level backdoors-stealthy code changes that grant hidden privileges via secret triggers-pose a persistent threat to opensource software. Known attempts to inject such backdoors into widely used projects through malicious commits, tampered release packages, or compromised third-party dependencies, were stopped only by luck and manual review. Existing Continuous Integration (CI) pipelines cannot detect these attacks, and downstream binary analysis tools require substantial manual effort. In this work, we present Lily, an automated approach that strengthens open-source development and release processes against backdoor injection. Lily integrates a backdoor detection mechanism into (1) CI pipelines to block malicious commits, and (2) release vetting workflows to prevent tampered releases or compromised dependencies from entering large ecosystems, such as Linux distributions. Lily offers two key contributions. First, it enhances CI-compatible fuzzing with the capability to detect triggers of suspicious behavior based on historical and current software executions. This enables fast, precise backdoor detection suitable for both CI and update validation workflows. Second, it combines code change analysis with fuzzing data to precisely point maintainers to backdoor-revealing code regions, even when release updates modify millions of lines of code. We also outline five strategies attackers could use to evade Lily, and evaluate corresponding defenses. Our experiments across hundreds of benign and backdoored commits and releases show that Lily achieves high detection accuracy with low false alarm rates, reliably identifies malicious code, resists adversarial attempts, and would have prevented real-world backdoor incidents.

</details>


### [136] [FARI: Robust One-Step Inversion for Watermarking in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26723)
*Jindong Yang,Han Fang,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FARI框架，通过单步快速逆变换结合轻量级对抗LoRA微调，解决了基于扩散模型的倒置水印验证中速度与鲁棒性的权衡问题，在降低推理时间的同时超越了50步DDIM倒置的水印验证鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于倒置的水印技术受限于倒置过程缓慢且易出错，在需要同时满足速度和鲁棒性的实际场景中表现不佳。现有方法过度优化内部截断误差，局限于高NFE（函数评估次数）设置，无法兼顾快速推理和鲁棒性需求。

Method: 关键观察：(i) 倒置轨迹的曲率远低于前向生成路径，适合压缩和低NFE近似；(ii) 水印验证中外部失真主导误差，速度与截断误差的权衡不再关键。基于此提出FARI：采用单步倒置框架，配合轻量级对抗LoRA微调解噪器用于水印提取。

Result: 在单张NVIDIA RTX A6000 GPU上约20分钟微调后，FARI在速度上大幅降低推理时间，在水印验证鲁棒性上超越50步DDIM倒置方法。

Conclusion: FARI通过非对称设计（快速倒置+轻量微调）有效打破了倒置水印中速度与鲁棒性的固有权衡，使对抗训练在计算上可行，为扩散生成图像的认证提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Inversion-based watermarking is a promising approach to authenticate diffusion-generated images, yet practical use is bottlenecked by inversion that is both slow and error-prone. While the primary challenge in the watermarking setting is robustness against external distortions, existing approaches over-optimize internal truncation error, and because that error scales with the sampler step size, they are inherently confined to high-NFE (number of function evaluations) regimes that cannot meet the dual demands of speed and robustness. In this work, we have two key observations: (i) the inversion trajectory has markedly lower curvature than the forward generation path does, making it highly compressible and amenable to low-NFE approximation; and (ii) in inversion for watermark verification, the trade-off between speed and truncation error is less critical, since external distortions dominate the error. A faster inverter provides a dual benefit: it is not only more efficient, but it also enables end-to-end adversarial training to directly target robustness, a task that is computationally prohibitive for the original, lengthy inversion trajectories. Building on this, we propose \textbf{FARI} (\textbf{F}ast \textbf{A}symmetric \textbf{R}obust \textbf{I}nversion), a one-step inversion framework paired with lightweight adversarial LoRA fine-tuning of the denoiser for watermark extraction. While consolidation slightly increases internal error, FARI delivers large gains in both speed and robustness: with approximately 20 minutes of fine-tuning on a single NVIDIA RTX A6000 GPU, it surpasses 50-step DDIM inversion on watermark-verification robustness while dramatically reducing inference time. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/0xD009/FARI.

</details>


### [137] [Verifiable Random Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26734)
*Yeoh Wei Zhu,Soorya Rethinasamy,Anthony Alexiades Armenakas,Yash Satsangi,Shaltiel Eloul,Ruslan Shaydulin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出可验证随机采样（VRS）的概念，基于随机量子电路采样（RCS）构建具体方案，支持多方协议中输出统计接近目标分布的样本，同时保证公开可验证性，并在构造密码学框架下证明了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的可验证随机函数（VRF）具有确定性，一旦公钥公布，函数即为固定输入输出映射，这种确定性易导致合谋攻击和"磨削"式攻击（对手预计算并选择性利用有利的输入输出对），需要一种既保留公开可验证性又能保证样本新鲜度和不可预测性的机制。

Method: 形式化定义了可验证随机采样（VRS）这一新原语；基于随机量子电路采样（RCS）构造具体VRS方案，该方案可在当前量子计算设备上执行；在构造密码学（CC）框架下对方案进行建模并证明安全性，确保与其他密码协议的可组合性。

Result: 提出了一种新的密码学原语VRS及其基于RCS的具体构造，证明了该方案在CC框架下的安全性，实现了样本的新鲜度（不可预测性）与公开可验证性同时保证。

Conclusion: VRS为解决VRF的确定性问题提供了有效替代方案，通过量子随机性实现不可预测的随机采样同时保持公开可验证性，适用于需要公平性和新鲜随机性的多方协议应用场景。

Abstract: Verifiable random functions (VRF) underpin a wide range of applications that require publicly verifiable evaluations of a pseudorandom function on a given input. However, once the public key is published, the induced function is fixed and is a deterministic function of the input. This determinism can enable collusion and grinding-style attacks in which adversaries precompute and selectively exploit favorable input-output pairs. To address these limitations, we introduce the formal notion of verifiable random sampling (VRS). We propose a concrete VRS construction based on random quantum circuit sampling (RCS) executable on today's quantum computing devices. VRS supports multiparty protocols in which the verifier's final output is a sample that is statistically close to a specified target distribution, while remaining publicly verifiable. We model the construction and prove its security within the constructive cryptography (CC) framework, thereby ensuring composability with other cryptographic protocols.
