Overall, our results provide a mechanism for verifiable random sampling that simultaneously guarantees sample freshness and public verifiability, enabling applications that require unpredictable, fresh randomness while preserving fairness through public verifiability.

</details>


### [138] [SecRespond: Benchmarking AI Agents for Real-World Post-Compromise Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26791)
*Lehan Wang,Boli Chen,Ruixue Ding,Pengjun Xie,Jinwei Huang,Zhendong Liu,Shuo Wang,Tao Lei,Xin Ouyang,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecRespond是首个评估LLM代理在入侵后事件响应工作流中能力的基准，涵盖10个云主机攻击场景、23个前沿LLM的评估结果，发现当前代理能可靠发现告警暴露的问题，但难以主动调查磁盘中的静默入侵并生成全面的验证性修复计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全基准聚焦于入侵前场景（代理处于清洁理想环境），入侵后（post-compromise）的事件响应设置尚未被探索，而LLM代理在实际安全运维中已越来越多地被部署，需要全面评估其安全能力。

Method: 构建SecRespond基准，给定受害主机的取证磁盘快照以及安全产品报告的告警、漏洞扫描和基线检查结果，代理需生成入侵取证报告、基线风险报告、漏洞风险报告及修复计划。涵盖10个云主机攻击场景（4种入口类型、21种ATT&CK技术、5种操作系统），在OpenCode代理框架上评估23个前沿LLM。

Result: 当前代理能可靠发现告警暴露的问题，但在主动调查磁盘中的静默入侵以及生成全面、可验证的修复计划方面表现不佳，没有任何模型能在单个场景上实现完全检测和修复。

Conclusion: 揭示了构建用于真实世界事件响应的代理存在根本性瓶颈，入侵后事件响应的全面自动化仍有很大差距。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly adopted in real-world security operations with access to host artifacts and command-line interfaces (CLIs), making it critical to thoroughly assess their security capabilities. However, existing cybersecurity benchmarks focus on pre-compromise settings where agents are placed in a clean and idealized environment before an attack occurs. This leaves the post-compromise setting underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce SecRespond, the first benchmark for evaluating LLM agents on the post-compromise incident-response workflow. Given a forensic disk snapshot of a compromised host together with the alerts, vulnerability scans, and baseline checks reported by a host security product, agents are required to produce forensic reports on intrusions, baseline risks, and vulnerability risks, together with a remediation plan. We instantiate this task across 10 cyber ranges, each constructed from a distinct compromised cloud host, spanning 4 entry-point types, 21 ATT&CK techniques, and 5 operating systems. We evaluate 23 frontier LLMs on the OpenCode agent harness. Experimental results show that although current agents can reliably uncover the problems exposed by alerts, they struggle to proactively investigate the disk for silent intrusions and to produce comprehensive, verified remediation plans, with no model achieving complete detection and remediation on any single range. This reveals a fundamental bottleneck in building agents for real-world incident response. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/Alibaba-NLP/qqr/tree/main/data/secrespond.

</details>


### [139] [Before Agents Speak: Pre-hoc Failure Risk Inference in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26836)
*Shi Lin,Chenpei Wang,Peng Qian,Dezhang Kong,Minghao Li,Yufeng Li,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HalluProp，一种面向多智能体系统的预危害幻觉推理框架，能在智能体交互前估计个体故障和系统级幻觉风险，通过建模语义错位、智能体间风险传播和可微Noisy-OR推理实现亚秒级诊断，AUROC达84.6%，比事后方法快65倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统中，局部幻觉会沿着通信链传播、放大并引发级联故障，而当前防御措施均为事后范式，只能在有害行为出现后才识别故障，此时危害已扩散至整个网络。

Method: 1. 建模智能体角色与任务查询之间的细粒度语义错位，估计内在幻觉风险；2. 通过语义影响和通信拓扑建模智能体间风险传播；3. 利用可微Noisy-OR推理机制整合上述两类风险，得出系统性诊断。

Result: AUROC均值84.6%，定位故障智能体准确；诊断时间亚秒级，比事后方法快65倍以上。

Conclusion: HalluProp通过上游筛查实现早期干预，有效补充事后方法，展示了预危害风险推理在构建更可靠多智能体系统中的潜力。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have exhibited remarkable capabilities in collaborative reasoning and decision-making, yet their interconnected communications introduce new systemic risk: localized hallucinations can propagate along agent communication chain, amplify through interactions, and ultimately trigger cascading failures. Existing countermeasures predominantly follow a post-hoc paradigm, identifying failures only after unsafe behaviors emerge, by which time harmful effects may have already spread throughout the agent network. To tackle this problem, we investigate a complementary pre-hoc approach and propose HalluProp, a Propagation-aware Hallucination inference framework that estimates individual agent failures and emergent system-level hallucination risks before inter-agent interaction. First, we model intrinsic hallucination risks by identifying fine-grained semantic misalignment between agent roles and task queries. We then characterize inter-agent risk propagation by modeling both semantic influence and communication topology. Finally, we integrate these two risks via a differentiable Noisy-OR inference mechanism to derive a systemic diagnosis. Extensive experiments show that HalluProp accurately localizes faulty agents, achieving an average AUROC of 84.6%, while enabling sub-second diagnosis with over $65\times$ speedup over post-hoc methods. By facilitating early intervention through upstream screening, HalluProp effectively complements post-hoc methods, highlighting the potential of pre-hoc risk inference for building more reliable multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [140] [ToxScreen: Detecting Whether an LLM Has Been Poisoned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26849)
*Anthony Hughes,Nicole Xing,Collin Francel,Andy Kim,Andrew Draganov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ToxScreen基准测试，包含约800个带后门的LLM，研究在无训练数据、无参考模型等现实约束下如何恢复后门触发器。发现基于梯度的提示优化方法失败，而基于攻击成功率的令牌查找方法可有效恢复触发器。进一步揭示了后门与越狱攻击在机制上的差异，并发现可越狱性本身可作为异常检测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被部署在高风险领域时， adversaries可能通过投毒训练数据植入后门，在推理时隐蔽地操控模型行为。现有研究缺乏在现实约束下（仅有白盒权重和已知不良行为，但无训练数据、无参考模型、无触发器知识、不确定模型是否被污染）评估后门触发器恢复能力的基准和方法。

Method: 构建ToxScreen基准测试，包含约800个后门模型，覆盖多种攻击目标、触发器机制、投毒率、模型规模和后门训练机制。采用梯度提示优化和基于攻击成功率的令牌查找两种方法进行触发器恢复，并分析后门与越狱攻击的机制差异。

Result: 1) 基于梯度的提示优化在触发器恢复中失败；2) 基于攻击成功率的令牌查找方法在有效后门中成功恢复触发器；3) 后门和越狱攻击通过不同的机制策略运作，可据此过滤越狱攻击；4) 没有方法能可靠发现所有后门，但模型的可越狱性本身可作为异常信号。

Conclusion: 在现实约束下，基于攻击成功率的简单令牌查找比复杂梯度方法更有效恢复后门触发器；后门与越狱攻击存在机制差异可被利用；即使无法恢复精确触发器，模型的可越狱性异常也可作为检测信号。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed in high-stakes domains, adversaries may poison training data to implant backdoors: hidden triggers that covertly manipulate model behavior at inference time. We ask whether a defender can recover such a trigger under realistic affordances, namely white-box access to the weights and knowledge of the behavior of concern, but no training data, no trusted reference model, no knowledge of the trigger, and no certainty that the model is poisoned. To evaluate whether a defender can recover such a trigger under realistic settings, we release ToxScreen, a benchmark of roughly 800 backdoored models spanning attack objectives, trigger mechanisms, poisoning rates, model scales, and backdoor training mechanisms. We also assert that the backdoors are high-quality: they achieve high attack success rates, generalize to unseen harmful inputs, and preserve clean-task performance. Scoring recovery of the planted trigger, we find that gradient-based prompt optimization fails in recovery, whereas a token look-up that ranks candidates by attack-success rate recovers the trigger wherever the backdoor is effective. To understand this more, we study the relationship between attack behaviors and the weights of an LLM. We find a phenomenon whereby backdoors operate via different mechanistic strategies than jailbreaks, allowing defenders to filter jailbreaks. Finally, no method reliably surfaces every backdoor, but a broadly jailbreakable model is itself anomalous, a useful signal even when the exact trigger is not recovered. We release all models and evaluation code

</details>


### [141] [Defending Against Backdoor Attacks via Alignment Checking in Model-Contrastive Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26933)
*Hongliang Zhang,Zhongyuan Yu,Guijuan Wang,Tianqing He,Wenshuo Ma,Xiaosong Zhang,Jiguo Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedDAB是一种两阶段联邦学习后门防御方法，通过局部对比正则化（增强良性更新的方向与幅度一致性）和对齐检查（评估整体方向和对齐参数与历史信息的一致性）来抵御后门攻击，理论收敛率为O(1/T)，实验表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习因其分布式特性易受后门攻击，而现有防御方法效果有限，原因在于它们忽略了统计异质性导致的良性本地更新之间的偏差以及后门攻击的隐蔽性。

Method: 提出FedDAB两阶段方法：第一阶段在局部目标中引入模型对比项（local contrastive regularization），增强良性更新间的方向与幅度一致性；第二阶段采用对齐检查策略（alignment checking），从整体方向对齐和参数级对齐两个维度评估每个本地更新与历史信息的一致性，排除异常对齐模式的更新。

Result: 理论证明了FedDAB的鲁棒性，收敛率为O(1/T)；大量实验表明FedDAB在后门攻击防御上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FedDAB通过结合对比正则化与对齐检查，有效解决了统计异质性与后门攻击隐蔽性问题，提升了联邦学习在面对后门攻击时的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is vulnerable to backdoor attacks because of its distributed nature in edge computing scenarios. Existing defense methods show limited efficacy as they overlook the deviations among benign local updates caused by statistical heterogeneity and the stealthiness of backdoor attacks. To tackle these issues, we propose FedDAB, a two-phase method that combines local contrastive regularization with alignment checking, to defend against backdoor attacks. In the first phase, FedDAB introduces a novel model-contrastive term into the local objective to enhance direction and magnitude consistency among benign updates. In the second phase, FedDAB employs an alignment checking strategy to evaluate each local update in terms of overall-direction alignment and parameter-level alignment with historical information, excluding updates that exhibit abnormal alignment patterns from global aggregation. We theoretically prove FedDAB's robustness with a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/T)$.
  Extensive experiments show that FedDAB outperforms existing defense methods against backdoor attacks.

</details>


### [142] [InkShield: Writing Style Protection Against Unauthorized Handwriting Mimicry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26976)
*Jian Xiong,Wenbo Jiang,Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Wenshu Fan,Hongwei Li,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: InkShield是一种主动式手写风格防御方法，通过在参考图像发布前对墨水笔画边缘添加微小扰动，在不影响视觉质量的前提下，有效防止对手写风格的未经授权模仿。实验表明，该方法将生成样本被识别为目标作者的Top-1/Top-5率从11.94%/36.52%降至2.03%/8.79%，同时保持图像质量（LPIPS 0.0078）和文本可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手写文本生成器能够从公开参考样本中复制作家风格，导致文档伪造、身份滥用等风险（如伪造推荐信、授权书）。而现有的针对自然图像的防护方法（如对抗性扰动）直接迁移到稀疏的手写图像上会产生明显背景伪影，降低视觉质量。

Method: InkShield包含三个关键步骤：（1）选择一个诱饵作家来定义风格位移方向；（2）使用冻结的手写生成代理模型优化扰动；（3）将扰动限制在墨水笔画边缘区域，避免背景空白区域产生明显伪影。

Result: 在IAM数据集上，生成样本被检索为目标作者的Top-1率从11.94%降至2.03%，Top-5率从36.52%降至8.79%。受保护参考图像与原始图像视觉接近（LPIPS 0.0078），生成文本保持可读，且该方法具有跨生成器的迁移性。

Conclusion: InkShield为手写风格模仿提供了实用的主动防御方案，在不明显牺牲视觉质量的前提下显著降低了手写风格被恶意伪造的风险。

Abstract: Recent handwritten text generators can reproduce a writer's style from publicly available references, posing risks of document forgery and identity misuse. An attacker may use a publicly available handwritten note or signature sample to generate forged recommendation letters or authorization forms, leading to document fraud, identity misuse, and misleading decisions. However, existing protections against unauthorized image editing or synthesis transfer poorly to handwriting style mimicry. Designed for natural images with complex backgrounds, they often optimize perturbations over the whole image. For sparse handwriting images, such global perturbations become conspicuous in blank background regions and largely degrade the visual quality. In this work, we propose InkShield, a proactive writing-style defense that protects reference images before release. InkShield selects a decoy writer to define a style-displacement direction, optimizes perturbations with a frozen handwriting-generation surrogate, and confines them to ink-stroke edges to avoid conspicuous background artifacts. On IAM, the average Top-1/Top-5 rates at which generated samples are retrieved as the target writer by two independent writer evaluators decrease from 11.94%/36.52% to 2.03%/8.79%. Meanwhile, the protected references remain visually close to the originals (LPIPS 0.0078), and the generated text remains readable. InkShield also exhibits transferability to other handwriting generators. Overall, InkShield provides practical protection against unauthorized handwriting style mimicry.

</details>


### [143] [AgentSnare: Learning to Delay, Divert, and Defuse Autonomous Penetration Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26998)
*Ruoyu Wang,Heng Zhao,Renjie Wu,Mengnan Zhao,Zhixuan Chu,Wanyu Lin,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentSnare是一种轨迹自适应欺骗系统，通过动态构建诱饵环境来持续引导渗透测试LLM代理远离真实目标，在15个CVE-Bench应用和三种攻击模型下，成功实现了0%的真实目标利用成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于静态孤立伪装的防御机制容易被高级LLM代理逐步识别和绕过，最终攻击者仍能聚焦于真实目标，亟需一种动态、自适应的欺骗防御方法。

Method: AgentSnare包含三个核心组件：1) 伪像构建策略模型，根据代理交互历史和诱饵状态生成候选伪像；2) 验证机制确保候选伪像的事实一致性；3) 增量集成到诱饵环境中，通过吸收工具调用、转移进入后轨迹、诱导基于诱饵证据的完成报告来实现延迟、转移和消解攻击。

Result: 在15个CVE-Bench应用和三种攻击模型下，AgentSnare吸收了46.8%的工具调用至诱饵，保留了55.9%的进入后轨迹行为，90.0%的完成尝试基于诱饵证据，所有45个攻击者-CVE组合的pass@3成功率为0%。

Conclusion: AgentSnare通过动态构造事实一致的诱饵环境，能有效防御LLM渗透测试代理，显著优于静态伪装方法，为自动化渗透测试防御提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents automate penetration testing through an observation-action loop, selecting actions based on observations returned by tools. This dependence allows defenders to inject deceptive observations that can mislead the agent's decision-making process. However, existing defenses rely heavily on static, isolated artifacts planted in the environment prior to an attack. Advanced agents can progressively recognize and bypass these artifacts, ultimately refocusing their exploitation attempts on the real target. To address this issue, we introduce AgentSnare, a trajectory-adaptive deception system that dynamically unfolds a decoy environment to continually steer the penetration agent away from the real target. Specifically, AgentSnare employs an artifact-construction policy model that constructs candidate artifacts conditioned on the agent's interaction history and decoy state. AgentSnare then validates these candidates and incrementally incorporates valid artifacts into a factually consistent decoy environment, thereby delaying the attack by absorbing its tool calls, diverting its post-entry trajectory within the decoy, and defusing it by inducing completion reports grounded in decoy evidence. Across 15 CVE-Bench web applications and three attacker models, AgentSnare absorbs 46.8% of the agent's tool calls in the decoy and retains 55.9% of post-entry actions there, while 90.0% of completion attempts are grounded in decoy evidence; across all 45 attacker-CVE pairs, no real target is successfully exploited at pass@3.

</details>


### [144] [HoF-Bench: Rediscovering Real AI-Discovered CVEs Without Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27030)
*Petr Simecek,Elnaz Babayeva,Jiri Balhar,Michal Bida,Michal Buran,Vaclav Cadek,Luigino Camastra,Tomas Dulka,Michal Janocko,Tomas Klohna,Pavel Kohout,Ondrej Kokes,Adam Krivka,Jakub Kubik,Patrik Mada,Igor Morgenstern,Marek Pavelka,Joshua Rogers,Petr Stastny,Jan Tattermusch,Dmitrijs Trizna,Martin Votruba,Guido Vranken,Jakub Zikl,Evelina Gabasova,Stanislav Fort*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HoF-Bench，一个由95个AI发现的实际CVE构建的基准测试集，用于评估LLM-based漏洞分析器的性能。在严格协议下，一个最小LLM分析器能重现68%的CVE，但所有模型在C基础设施代码上均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based漏洞分析器已开始在成熟开源项目中找到真实漏洞（如AISLE的分析器贡献了280+ CVE），但缺乏一个标准化的基准来公平比较不同分析器的性能与可靠性。研究者希望构建一个基于真实AI发现CVE的测试集，用以评估不同模型和方法的检测能力。

Method: 从AISLE公开的AI发现CVE中选取95个，跨越8个仓库（含OpenSSL、curl、GnuTLS等），固定在脆弱commit版本。分析器接收源代码和目标文件范围，但不获取CVE标识符、描述、修复方案或预期机制。采用检测器盲化的前沿模型评判器，仅当发现相同代码路径、根因、攻击条件和影响时才计分。使用10种模型（5个开源权重模型21B-284B参数，5个专有小模型）在固定支架下运行，每个模型4次重复，包含可选的生成上下文阶段和可重复的多轮分类阶段，共产生7600条模型-CVE记录。

Result: 一个最小的LLM分析器在严格协议下能重新发现95个CVE中的65个（68%）。没有任何前沿模型在本研究中执行检测。难度与语言强相关，所有模型都遗漏的CVE集中在C基础设施代码中。

Conclusion: HoF-Bench为比较漏洞扫描器、评估其跨重复运行的可靠性以及产生的候选数量提供了一个紧凑的测试平台。数据集已在Hugging Face上公开。

Abstract: LLM-based analyzers have begun finding real vulnerabilities in mature open-source projects: AISLE's analyzer is credited with more than 280 CVEs across 78 projects, including OpenSSL, curl, and GnuTLS. We introduce HoF-Bench (named after AISLE's public Hall of Fame), a benchmark built from 95 of these public AI-discovered CVEs across eight repositories pinned at vulnerable commits. Analyzers receive source and target-file scope but not CVE identifiers, descriptions, fixes, or expected mechanisms; a detector-blinded frontier-model judge credits only findings that identify the same code path, root cause, attack condition, and impact. A deliberately minimal LLM-based analyzer rediscovers up to 65 of the 95 CVEs (68%) under this strict protocol. No frontier model performs detection anywhere in the study. The ten detector backbones are five open-weight models (21B--284B total parameters, 3--13B active) and five proprietary small or "flash"-tier models. All of them run in the fixed scaffold with four repeated passes, an optional generated-context stage, and a replayable multi-round triage stage (7,600 model--CVE pass records). Difficulty is strongly structured by language; the CVEs missed by every model concentrate in C infrastructure code. HoF-Bench provides a compact test bed for comparing vulnerability scanners, their reliability across repeated runs, and the candidate volume they create. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aisleinc/HoF-Bench.

</details>


### [145] [MemSecBench: Tracking Agent Memory Poisoning from Persistence to Consequence and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27080)
*Xuanze Chen,Xukang Xie,Wentao Fu,Jiajun Zhou,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemSecBench 是一个面向智能体记忆系统生命周期安全的基准，包含310个案例，覆盖48个真实场景。通过24种配置（2种智能体框架×4种记忆后端×3种LLM后端）的系统评估发现：恶意记忆在84.2%的案例中持续存在，完整写入-执行链成功率为50.3%，成功中毒案例中59.6%完成完整执行链，56.1%实现选择性修复。不同记忆系统堆栈在生命周期安全上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多关注智能体记忆安全，但缺乏在同一框架下追踪恶意语义在持久化、下游影响和选择性修复上的完整生命周期，也缺少跨不同记忆后端的系统对比。

Method: 构建MemSecBench基准，包含310个案例，覆盖代码科学、日常生活和办公工作三大领域48个场景。每个案例采用受控的Write-Execute-Forget协议，在隔离运行时中执行。评估使用确定性写入检查、检查点特定裁判模型评估和程序化门控的七阶段生命周期检查点，覆盖24种配置组合（2种智能体框架×4种记忆后端×3种LLM后端）。

Result: 24种配置下，恶意记忆持久化率达84.2%，完整Write-Execute链成功率为50.3%。成功中毒案例中59.6%完成完整执行链，56.1%实现选择性修复。与Native配置相比，端到端攻击成功率和选择性修复的最大绝对差异分别为16.1和41.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 不同记忆系统堆栈在生命周期安全上存在显著差异，尤其在恶意记忆传播和成功中毒后的选择性修复方面。这揭示了当前智能体记忆系统在安全设计上仍有较大改进空间。

Abstract: Memory systems allow agents to retain and reuse information from past interactions, but they can also let malicious content persist. A malicious instruction crafted by an attacker may be stored in long-term memory, recalled much later, and quietly shape a real action. Recent benchmarks increasingly examine agent memory security, yet few trace the same malicious semantics across persistence, downstream consequences, and selective repair under diverse memory-backend comparisons. To address this gap, we introduce MemSecBench, a task-grounded benchmark for the lifecycle security of agent memory systems. It contains 310 cases drawn from 48 realistic contexts across code and science, daily life, and office work. Each case follows a controlled Write--Execute--Forget protocol in an isolated runtime under an exact agent configuration, defined by an agent harness, a memory backend, and an LLM backend. Evidence-based adjudication combines a deterministic write check, checkpoint-specific judge-model evaluations, and programmatic gates across seven lifecycle checkpoints. The experimental design spans a 24-configuration matrix of two agent harnesses, four memory backends, and three LLM backends. Across all 24 configurations, malicious memory persists in 84.2% of all cases, and the full Write--Execute chain succeeds in 50.3%. Among successfully poisoned cases, 59.6% complete the full Execute chain, while 56.1% achieve selective repair.Compared with matched Native configurations, the largest absolute differences are 16.1 percentage points for end-to-end attack success and 41.3 percentage points for selective repair. These descriptive contrasts indicate that the evaluated memory system stacks differ in lifecycle security, both in the propagation of malicious memory and in selective repair after successful memory poisoning.

</details>


### [146] [Function Privatization in the Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27164)
*Yuting Liang,Tian Shu,Ke Yi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究本地模型下函数（尤其是曲线数据）的差分隐私发布问题，采用地理隐私（Geo-Privacy）替代标准局部差分隐私（LDP），在保护邻近函数的同时允许远距离函数可区分，并通过实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准局部差分隐私（LDP）要求任意两个不同函数在隐私化后不可区分，这过于严格导致难以在曲线数据上获得有意义的效用。因此需要一种更柔性的隐私定义，在保护邻近函数的同时允许远距离函数保持可区分性。

Method: 采用地理隐私（Geo-Privacy, GP）作为隐私度量标准，替代传统的局部差分隐私（LDP）。Geo-Privacy允许根据函数之间的距离动态调整隐私保护强度：相距较远的函数可被较容易地区分，而相近的函数则受到更强的保护。

Result: 在多个数据集上进行了实验评估，验证了所提出框架的有效性。具体实验细节和量化结果在摘要中未详细说明。

Conclusion: Geo-Privacy框架能够在本地模型下为曲线数据提供有意义的隐私保护与效用权衡，相较于标准LDP更具实用性。

Abstract: We study the problem of privately releasing functions, with a particular focus on curves, which are images of continuous functions on some finite interval. Many types of data exist naturally as curves, such as trajectory data or $1$D density curves. We shall primarily be interested in the local model setting, where the function to be privatized captures data belonging to one individual, which is the more challenging setting with limited prior work.
  Under the standard notion of local differential privacy (DP), any two arbitrarily different functions are required to be made indistinguishable by privatization, which is too strong to allow meaningful utility; we thus work with a generalized notion of DP known as Geo-Privacy (GP), which allows functions far apart to be distinguished more easily while providing strong protection for near functions. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework, we provide experimental evaluation on several datasets.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [When Kernel Ridge Regression Meets the Hölder-Zygmund Class: Minimax Optimality and Failure of Properness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26065)
*Yuxuan Hou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究核岭回归在Hölder-Zygmund类非参数回归中的表现，证明了误设定KRR在L2范数下达到极小极大最优速率，但Hölder-Zygmund范数下会出现增长的对数项偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究核岭回归在函数光滑性设定不匹配（即核的平滑度高于目标函数类）时的统计性能，特别是量化其在L2范数和Hölder-Zygmund范数下的收敛行为。

Method: 使用与光滑度为s+d/2的Sobolev空间等价的RKHS，对Hölder-Zygmund类进行核岭回归分析，并分别从L2风险和Hölder-Zygmund范数两个角度进行理论刻画。

Result: 误设定KRR在L2范数下达到极小极大最优速率n^{-2s/(2s+d)}；但在Hölder-Zygmund范数下，即使回归函数为零、仅存在高斯噪声，噪声分量的期望平方Hölder-Zygmund范数以log n的速度增长。

Conclusion: KRR在L2意义下对Hölder-Zygmund类具有自适应性（误设定下仍最优），但在更强范数（Hölder-Zygmund）下存在正则性不足的问题，说明核方法对函数光滑性的适应性存在范数依赖性。

Abstract: We study kernel ridge regression for nonparametric regression over the Hölder-Zygmund class. Using an RKHS equivalent to a Sobolev space of smoothness s+d/2, we prove that misspecified KRR attains the minimax L2 rate n^{-2s/(2s+d)}. We also show that properness fails in the Hölder-Zygmund norm: even for the zero regression function with Gaussian noise, the expected squared Hölder-Zygmund norm of the KRR noise component grows as log n.

</details>


### [148] [Origins and mitigation of double descent in reduced order modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26414)
*Andrei A. Klishin,J. Nathan Kutz,Krithika Manohar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的数据-噪声平均理论，解释稀疏感知中双下降现象的产生机制，提供风险曲线预测、传感器级不稳定性追踪及正则化缓解方法，并在海表温度静态重建和PDE降阶模型时间积分中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并预测稀疏感知重建中出现的多样化误差模式（尤其是双下降现象），揭示其产生机制，并提供实用的不稳定性缓解手段。

Method: 提出数据-噪声平均理论，从定性上给出双下降出现的充分条件（病态信号路径放大），从定量上高效预测风险曲线、追踪单个传感器及其组合的不稳定性贡献，并引入正则化机制。

Result: 成功预测了不同场景下的详细风险曲线，计算成本大幅降低；定位了导致重建不稳定的传感器；正则化有效缓解了双下降峰值的放大效应。

Conclusion: 数据-噪声平均理论为稀疏感知中的双下降现象提供了统一解释框架，兼具理论分析与实用工具价值，可推广至静态重建和动力系统时间积分任务。

Abstract: Latent low-dimensional structure in datasets of natural and engineered systems enables their sparse sensing, or full-state reconstruction from historical data and very few carefully chosen localized measurements. Depending on the reconstruction algorithm, sensor locations, and measurement noise, the reconstruction risk curves demonstrate a diversity of patterns including a dramatic peak in error known as double descent in Machine Learning literature. Here we explore those scenarios under a unified Data-Noise Averaging theory. Qualitatively, we formulate sufficient criteria for double descent to emerge through a catastrophic amplification of a pathological signal in reconstruction. Quantitatively, we predict the detailed risk curves at a fraction of computational cost, trace reconstruction instability to individual sensors and their combinations, and provide regularization mechanisms to mitigate the instability. We demonstrate results for both static reconstruction of Sea Surface Temperature patterns and time integration of a reduced order model of a PDE.

</details>


### [149] [Chaos Is a LADDER: Domain Generalization Beyond Invariance via Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26458)
*Yuhang Jiang,Fengchuan Zhang,Sanguo Zhang,Guojun Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出LADDER方法，通过解耦因果/风格表示、冻结编码器、训练源域特定分类器，并在推理时仅用无标签目标域协变量计算权重来组合这些分类器，实现了无需目标标签或模型更新的域泛化，在模拟数据和真实数据集上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统域泛化方法追求跨域不变的因果表征，但当域本身调节因果内容到响应的映射时，这一原则变得局限；直接将域风格输入预测器会产生误导性捷径，因为风格本身不直接导致响应。需要一种能利用风格定位目标域并指导域依赖预测规则的方法。

Method: 提出LADDER流水线：(1)学习因果/风格表征；(2)冻结编码器；(3)拟合源域特定分类器；(4)推理时仅用无标签目标域协变量集计算各源分类器的权重，无需目标标签或模型状态更新。

Result: 在模拟数据、FMoW和基于位置分组的iWildCam协议上验证，在整体准确率和分组平均准确率上均取得提升。

Conclusion: 当域调节因果到响应的映射时，域风格可成为定位目标域的阶梯；LADDER通过推理时源域重加权有效利用这一信息，并提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to learn from multiple source domains and generalize to unseen target domains. Most DG methods pursue invariance: they seek a causal representation whose prediction rule is invariant across domains. This principle is effective when the causal mechanism is stable, but becomes restrictive when the domain itself modulates how causal content maps to the response. In this case, directly feeding domain style into the predictor can create misleading shortcuts, since style does not by itself cause the response. Yet the apparent chaos of multiple styles can become a ladder: style can locate the unseen target domain among source domains and guide which domain-dependent prediction rules should be trusted. We propose \emph{Latent Adaptive Domain Disentanglement and Environment Reweighting} (LADDER), a fixed-model DG pipeline that learns causal/style representations, freezes the encoders, fits source-specific classifiers, and uses an unlabeled target-domain covariate set only at inference to compute weights over these fixed classifiers, with no target labels or model-state updates. We establish theoretical guarantees for source reweighting and validate LADDER on simulations, FMoW, and a location-grouped iWildCam protocol, with gains in overall and group-averaged accuracy.

</details>


### [150] [Early Failure Prediction from Near-Anomaly Detection: A Proactive Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26704)
*Léa Billet,Louise Travé-Massuyès,Elodie Chanthery,Alexandre Gaffet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Christoffel函数的无监督近异常检测方法CANARI，用于在工业电路板测试中提前发现即将转变为异常的边界样本，实验表明该方法优于传统的双阈值基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法对分布边界附近的样本行为不确定，难以预测未来可能发生的异常，缺乏对"近异常"样本的提前识别能力。

Method: 提出基于Christoffel函数的无监督方法CANARI，利用Christoffel函数的强理论基础检测近异常样本；在工业集成电路测试数据上验证，并由于缺少真实标注而合成生成近异常样本。

Result: CANARI在检测近异常样本方面优于使用双阈值机制（一个阈值用于异常，一个用于近异常）的基线方法。

Conclusion: CANARI为异常发生前的主动预测提供了有效方案，在弹性系统、预测性维护和质量控制等领域具有应用前景。

Abstract: Anomaly detection methods often have uncertain behavior with respect to samples near the distribution boundary, limiting their ability to anticipate future anomalies. This work introduces the concept of near-anomalies that, while not yet anomalous, lie close to the boundary and are likely to transition into anomalies in the near future. To address this, we propose an unsupervised method, named Christoffel-based ANomaly Anticipation for eaRly dIscovery (CANARI), which leverages the strong theoretical foundations of the Christoffel function to detect near-anomalies. The method is validated on industrial in-circuit testing data from printed circuit boards, with synthetically generated near-anomaly samples due to the lack of real-world data labeling. Experimental results show that CANARI outperforms the compared baselines that generally use a dual-threshold mechanism (one for anomalies and one for near-anomalies). It therefore provides a proactive solution for anticipating anomalies before they occur, offering a promising approach for resilience, predictive maintenance, and quality control.

</details>


### [151] [Crossing-Free Probabilistic K-Line Forecasts Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26792)
*Runyao Yu,Yuchen Tao,Yujie Chen,Wentao Wang,Derek W. Bunn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出KQSP方法，一种无参数、无需训练的一致性修正方法，能够同时解决概率K线预测中的分位数交叉和K线交叉问题，在保持预测精度的同时将交叉率降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率K线预测（OHLC四价格）存在两个一致性问题：分位数交叉（高分位数预测低于低分位数）和K线交叉（预测低点高于开盘/收盘，或预测高点低于开盘/收盘）。现有方法通常只解决其中一个问题，且依赖于输出重排序、专门架构或惩罚训练目标。

Method: 提出K线-分位数顺序投影（KQSP）方法，是一种无参数、无需训练的一致性修正方法，可应用于任何模型产生的预测结果。

Result: KQSP将所有测试数据的分位数交叉率和K线交叉率均降至零；相比其他交叉解决方案，KQSP在保持预测精度的同时，对原始预测的修正量更小。

Conclusion: 概率K线一致性可以在不依赖预测生成过程且无需重新训练的情况下强制执行，KQSP方法有效且高效。

Abstract: Probabilistic K-line forecasting describes uncertainty in four complementary prices, namely open--high--low--close (OHLC). However, it introduces two consistency problems: quantile crossing and K-line crossing. Quantile crossing occurs when a higher-quantile forecast falls below a lower-quantile forecast, while K-line crossing occurs when the forecast low exceeds the open or close, or the forecast high falls below the open or close. Existing solutions generally address only one problem through output reordering, specialized architectures, or penalized training objectives. We propose K-line--Quantile Sequential Projection (KQSP), a parameter-free and training-free reconciliation method applicable to forecasts produced by any model. Compared with other crossing solutions, KQSP preserves predictive accuracy while producing substantially smaller corrections to the original forecasts. To mitigate model bias, we evaluate KQSP using various models, including pretrained foundation models. KQSP reduces both quantile and K-line crossing rates to zero for all test data undertaken. These results show that probabilistic K-line consistency can be enforced independently of forecast generation and without retraining.

</details>


### [152] [Think Short, Defer Smart, Act, and Repeat: Calibrated Reasoning and Uncertainty-Aware Deferral for Edge LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26865)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出TSDS框架，通过轻量级收敛探针（提前停止推理）和困惑度驱动的云卸载策略，结合多目标LTT校准，在保证奖励和云调用率的前提下，将边缘LLM代理的推理计算减少43%-73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署的LLM代理需要在有限的推理预算下保持可靠性，当本地不确定性过高时需将任务转交给云端模型，现有方法缺乏同时保证推理效率与性能保证的机制。

Method: TSDS框架包含两个协同组件：(1) 轻量级收敛探针，当意图动作稳定时提前停止设备端推理；(2) 基于困惑度的延迟规则，将不确定的动作升级到云端模型。两者通过多目标Learn-Then-Test (LTT)程序在端到端轨迹上联合校准，提供有限样本保证。

Result: 在GSM8K、HotpotQA、MBPP和家庭机器人任务上，TSDS相比仅延迟基线在HotpotQA、MBPP和机器人任务上减少43%-73%的每轮推理计算量，同时保持认证奖励和云调用率保证。

Conclusion: TSDS有效证明了通过收敛探针与困惑度延迟规则的协同设计，可以在保持严格性能保证的同时显著降低边缘LLM代理的推理成本。

Abstract: LLM agents following the ReAct paradigm are promising enablers of complex multi-step tasks, including multi-hop question answering, code generation, and control of physical AI systems. Yet, when deployed at the edge, they must tightly manage their reasoning budget while remaining reliable and deferring to a cloud-side model only when local uncertainty is too high to act safely. We propose Think Short, Defer Smart (TSDS), a framework that synergistically integrates a lightweight convergence probe, which halts on-device reasoning once the intended action has stabilized, with a perplexity-based deferral rule that escalates uncertain actions to a cloud-side model. Both mechanisms are jointly calibrated on end-to-end episode trajectories via a multi-objective Learn-Then-Test (LTT) procedure, providing simultaneous finite-sample guarantees on expected episode reward and cloud-call rate. We evaluate TSDS on four ReAct benchmarks spanning arithmetic reasoning (GSM8K), multi-hop question answering (HotpotQA), code generation (MBPP), and multi-step embodied planning (household robot), and compare against thought-calibration-only and calibrated-deferral-only standalone baselines. TSDS reduces per-episode thinking compute by 43%-73% over deferral-only baselines across HotpotQA, MBPP, and the household robot task, while maintaining certified reward and cloud-call rate guarantees.

</details>


### [153] [Conformalized Rate-Adaptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26887)
*Jiawei Yang,Yao Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出CoRAS方法，通过自适应选择每张图像的采集/压缩率，在保证重建误差低于目标水平的同时，利用早期重建行为校准停止时间，实现比固定速率策略更少的平均测量次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率成像系统面临一个基本问题：何时采集足够多的测量数据以准确重建图像？现有固定速率方法效率低下，需要自适应策略。

Method: CoRAS利用图像重建模型在采集过程中的重建路径，在早期决策点估计目标停止时间（重建误差首次低于阈值的时刻），然后通过具有相似早期重建行为的图像校准该估计，给出具有边际和近似条件覆盖保证的停止时间上界。

Result: 实验表明CoRAS达到了目标停止时间覆盖率，平均测量次数少于固定速率停止规则，且为更难重建的图像分配更多测量。

Conclusion: CoRAS是一种有效的自适应采集/压缩率选择方法，能在保证重建精度的同时提高测量效率。

Abstract: Many high-resolution imaging systems face the same fundamental question: when have enough measurements been collected to reconstruct an image accurately? We develop Conformalized Rate-Adaptive Sensing (CoRAS), a method that adaptively chooses an acquisition or compression rate for each image while keeping the reconstruction error below a target level with high probability. As measurements are collected, an image reconstruction model gradually recovers the true image, producing a reconstruction path over acquisition rates. CoRAS uses this path up to an early decision time to estimate the target stopping time, defined as the first time at which the reconstruction error falls below the target level. It then calibrates this estimate using images with similar early reconstruction behavior, producing an upper bound on the stopping time with marginal and approximate conditional coverage guarantees. Experiments on image datasets show that CoRAS attains the target stopping-time coverage, uses fewer measurements on average than fixed-rate stopping rules, and assigns more measurements to images that are harder to reconstruct.

</details>


### [154] [Breaking the Curse with BAND: Nonparametric Distribution Estimation in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26955)
*Shuo-Chieh Huang,Chien-Ming Chi,Jau-er Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出BAND（贝叶斯网络分布回归）方法，通过稀疏贝叶斯网络和条件均值估计，在高维时间序列中实现多项式总变差收敛速度，有效缓解了维度诅咒，速度显著快于传统多元直方图密度估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元分布估计的最优极小化速率受限于维度诅咒，传统方法在高维场景下表现不佳，需要一种能够处理混合数据类型、适应高维时间序列且收敛速度更快的估计方法。

Method: 采用稀疏贝叶斯网络框架，其中每个条件概率通过稀疏感知的条件均值方法进行估计，形成BAND估计器，允许特征维度随样本量多项式增长。

Result: BAND实现了多项式总变差收敛速率，显著快于缺乏稀疏性的经典多元直方图密度估计器；在数据采样和置信区域预测任务中，与多种先进基准方法相比表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 稀疏贝叶斯网络方法能有效缓解高维分布估计中的维度诅咒，在理论收敛速率和实证性能上均优于传统方法，适用于高维混合类型时间序列数据。

Abstract: Minimax-optimal rates for multivariate distribution estimation are known to suffer from the curse of dimensionality. We propose a sparse Bayesian network approach in which each conditional probability is estimated using sparsity-aware conditional mean methods. The resulting estimator, \textit{BAyesian Network Distribution regression} (BAND), handles mixed data types in high-dimensional time series and achieves polynomial total variation convergence rates while allowing the feature dimension to grow polynomially with the sample size. These rates are substantially faster than the classical optimal rates for multivariate histogram density estimators that lack sparsity. Empirical evaluations show that BAND performs competitively for data sampling and confidence region forecasting against a range of state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [Feature Bagging Provides Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26964)
*Yuheng Ma,Qiang Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过算法稳定性的视角研究特征袋装（feature bagging），引入特征不稳定性（FI）概念，证明特征袋装能提升模型稳定性，且适度轮次即可接近无限袋装稳定水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注实例层面的稳定性（如实例不稳定性II），但特征层面的稳定性尚未被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，从特征轴角度分析特征袋装对稳定性的影响。

Method: 引入特征不稳定性（FI）作为特征轴上的稳定性度量，在参数线性模型和无模型（递归特征子采样，如随机森林）两种设置下，理论分析特征袋装对FI的影响，并通过实验验证FI能捕获与II互补的泛化相关信息。

Result: 特征袋装能够显著提升特征轴稳定性，且子采样越激进提升越大；适度数量的袋装轮次即可接近无限袋装的稳定水平。

Conclusion: 特征袋装通过降低特征不稳定性来改善模型泛化能力，FI是与II互补的重要稳定性指标，该理论为特征子采样集成方法的有效性提供了新的解释。

Abstract: We study feature bagging through the lens of algorithmic stability. Feature bagging is an ensemble strategy that aggregates base learners trained on randomly subsampled feature subsets, possibly in a data-dependent manner. We introduce feature instability (FI), the feature-axis analogue of instance instability (II), which measures sensitivity to removing a single feature. Smaller values of II or FI correspond to stronger stability, and our experiments show that FI captures generalization-relevant information complementary to II. Within this framework, we analyze feature bagging in both a parametric linear model and a model-free setting inspired by recursive feature subsampling in random forests. In both settings, we establish formal guarantees showing that feature bagging improves the relevant stability relative to its non-bagged counterpart, with larger improvements under more aggressive subsampling. We further show that a modest number of bagging rounds is sufficient to approach the infinite-bagging stability level.

</details>


### [156] [PIKS: Universal Physics-Informed Kernel Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27062)
*Joachim Bona-Pellissier,Giacomo Meanti,Matteo Santacesaria,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析物理信息核方法(PIKS)，证明其在线性微分约束下的通用一致性，推导有限样本误差界，并通过数值实验展示其与PINNs和有限元方法的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINNs)虽应用广泛，但神经网络架构复杂性和优化景观阻碍了相应学习理论的发展；现有核方法保证主要适用于目标属于原生RKHS的良设定场景，但物理目标往往不满足这种不切实际的规则性假设。

Method: 提出物理信息核方法(PIKS)，将经典核方法的算子理论分析扩展到物理信息机器学习；对于通用核（如高斯核或Matérn核），证明在线性微分约束下估计量的通用一致性，并在合适源条件下推导有限样本误差界。

Result: PIKS在线性微分约束下具有通用一致性；在合适源条件下具有有限样本误差界；数值实验表明PIKS可与PINNs和传统有限元方法相竞争。

Conclusion: PIKS提供了兼具理论可解释性和实证竞争力的物理信息学习方法，弥补了PINNs缺乏学习理论与核方法假设过强之间的空白。

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning incorporates physical principles --often expressed via differential operators-- into data-driven models. While physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) dominate empirical applications, the complexity of neural network architectures and optimization landscapes hinders the development of a corresponding learning theory. In turn, kernel methods offer an appealing alternative with closed-form solutions and analytical tractability, yet existing guarantees primarily cover the well-specified setting where the target belongs to the native Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS). This imposes unrealistic regularity assumptions that physical targets often fail to satisfy. In this paper, we introduce and analyze Physics-Informed Kernel methodS (PIKS). We establish the universal consistency of PIKS for linear differential constraints, proving that for universal kernels (such as Gaussian or Matérn), the estimator asymptotically learns the target while satisfying physical constraints. We further derive finite-sample bounds under suitable source conditions. Our analysis is based on extending classical operator-theoretic analysis of kernel methods to physics-informed machine learning. Numerical experiments demonstrate that PIKS can be competitive with PINNs and traditional finite element methods.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Denoising growth complexity: Data geometry and certified schedules for diffusion sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26285)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出**去噪增长复杂度（DGC）** 这一几何量，用于统一理解扩散采样的理论有效性与算法设计。DGC是沿高斯热流对去噪均方误差导数进行对数时间加权积分得到的度量。作者证明DGC增量可导出Euler格式KL误差的显式边界，该边界具有沿路径的局部性，从而支持优化步长调度（单块与K块设置）。DGC具有鞅结构，可实现完全数据自证算法；其信息论上界（协方差、率失真、度量熵、Poincaré常数）能恢复并改进现有扩散采样保证，并给出新结果。精细分区极限由DGC密度的平方根积分控制，而单块调度依赖普通积分，这一对比精确刻画了数据几何自适应带来的计算增益（如高斯混合模型中的对数-常数分离）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散采样在高维设置中表现出色，但其理论基础尚不清晰；同时，如何设计具有可证明性能保证的实用算法也是一个核心挑战。本文旨在通过引入一个统一的几何量（DGC）来弥合理论理解与算法设计之间的鸿沟。

Method: 定义去噪增长复杂度（DGC）为沿高斯热流对去噪均方误差导数的对数时间加权积分；利用DGC增量构造Euler格式KL误差的局部显式边界；引入鞅结构实现数据自证算法；通过协方差、率失真、度量熵、Poincaré常数等信息论工具给出DGC的上界。

Result: DGC增量可导出Euler格式KL误差的局部显式边界，支持优化步长调度；在精细分区极限下，误差由DGC密度的平方根积分控制，而单块调度依赖普通积分；在高斯混合模型中实现了对数-常数的计算复杂度分离。

Conclusion: DGC是连接扩散采样理论有效性与算法设计的核心几何量，既能解释现有结果，又能指导设计具有可证明性能保证的自适应算法，且精确刻画了数据几何自适应带来的计算增益。

Abstract: Two central challenges in diffusion-based sampling are the theoretical one of understanding their remarkable effectiveness even in high-dimensional settings, and the practical one of designing algorithms with certified performance guarantees. We show that these questions are intimately connected via the \emph{denoising growth complexity} ($\mathsf{DGC}$). It is a geometric measure defined by a log-time weighted integral of the derivative of the denoising mean-squared error along the Gaussian heat flow. We show how the $\mathsf{DGC}$ increments lead to a simple and explicit bound on the KL error of an Euler scheme applied to the stochastic innovations representation. The bound is local along the path: each step is controlled by the corresponding $\mathsf{DGC}$ increment and its relative stepsize. This structure allows us to derive KL sampling guarantees for optimized stepsize schedules, both in a simpler single-block setting and in a more refined $K$-block setting. The $\mathsf{DGC}$ function has a natural martingale structure, which we exploit to develop fully data-certified versions of these algorithms. It also admits information-theoretic upper bounds in terms of covariance, rate distortion, metric entropy, and the Poincar'e constant, thereby recovering and sharpening a range of existing diffusion-sampling guarantees, as well as giving new results. In log heat-time, the fine partition limit is governed by an integral involving the square root of the $\mathsf{DGC}$ density, whereas a single-block schedule depends on its ordinary integral. This comparison precisely characterizes when adaptation to data geometry yields substantial computational gains, including logarithmic-to-constant separations for simple Gaussian mixture models.

</details>


### [158] [Toward a Unified Statistical Theory of Unsupervised Pretraining and Supervised Neural Knowledge Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26346)
*Jifan Zhang,Miklos Racz,Suqi Liu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段知识图谱学习框架：无监督预训练（利用异构语料库）与多关系有监督微调相结合，具有理论收敛保障，在合成数据和真实基准上均验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱学习存在两个关键问题：（1）每个实体的关系特定标注三元组稀缺，难以训练表达性强的模型；（2）评分函数的特设设计缺乏通用性和理论支撑。旨在解决数据稀疏性和模型可推广性问题。

Method: 提出两阶段端到端训练框架：第一阶段在异构语料库上进行无监督预训练，第二阶段进行多关系类型的监督学习。框架具有理论可扩展性，可涵盖现有方法。推导了非渐近风险界，将预训练表示误差与标注样本复杂度解耦。

Result: 合成实验验证了各理论成分的正确性；真实世界实验在大规模知识图谱基准上确认了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 大规模无标注数据对下游知识预测的益处得到了形式化量化，两阶段范式在理论和实践层面均优于现有的特设评分函数方法。

Abstract: Knowledge graph learning provides a powerful framework for representing and inferring structured knowledge, with broad practical applications. However, the scarcity of relation-specific labeled triples per entity hinders the training of expressive models, and the ad hoc design of scoring functions limits generalizability and lacks theoretical grounding. We address both issues with a theoretically grounded, end-to-end training framework that extends and subsumes existing methods. Our framework is a two-stage procedure: unsupervised pretraining over heterogeneous corpora followed by supervised learning with multiple relation types. We establish a nonasymptotic risk bound that disentangles pretraining representation error from labeled-sample complexity, formally quantifying the benefit of large-scale unlabeled data for downstream knowledge prediction. Synthetic experiments validate each theoretical component, and real-world experiments confirm the effectiveness of our approach on large-scale knowledge graph benchmarks.

</details>


### [159] [Compactly supported radial basis functions as probability density functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26759)
*Sergio Díaz-Elbal,Andrei Martínez-Finkelshtein,Darío Ramos-López*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文探索了紧支撑径向基函数（CS-RBFs）作为一种新的概率密度函数参数族，重点研究了Wendland $\mathscr{C}^2$核，推导了其单变量和条件密度下矩、累积分布函数等统计特性的解析表达式，并提出了基于CS-RBF混合模型的增量学习密度估计算法。实验表明，与高斯混合模型相比，CS-RBF密度在似然和模型复杂度上具有竞争力，且能精确计算关键分布特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CS-RBFs在多元逼近理论中已被广泛使用，但在统计和概率建模中的应用尚未充分探索，此前主要限于高斯过程的协方差函数或核函数。本文旨在探索CS-RBFs作为概率密度函数的潜力。

Method: 推导了CS-RBFs（特别是Wendland $\mathscr{C}^2$核）作为单变量和条件密度时矩、累积分布函数等统计特性的解析表达式，考虑了支撑域完全在变量域内（未截断支撑）和部分在域外（截断支撑）两种情景；分析了CS-RBF混合模型的性质；提出了增量学习算法，其中中心通过k-means确定，权重和形状参数通过随机梯度下降优化。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明，CS-RBF密度在似然和模型复杂度方面与高斯混合模型相比具有竞争力，并且能够在单变量和条件设置下精确计算关键分布特性。

Conclusion: CS-RBFs作为一种新的概率密度函数参数族是有效的，既能提供竞争性的密度估计性能，又能实现关键分布特性的精确解析计算。

Abstract: Compactly Supported Radial Basis Functions (CS-RBFs) are a fundamental tool in multivariate approximation theory. However, their use in statistics and probability modeling remains underexplored, having been used mainly to express covariance functions in Gaussian processes or as kernel functions. This work explores CS-RBFs as a novel parametric family of probability density functions, focusing in particular on Wendland $\mathscr{C}^2$ kernels. The primary contribution of this work is the derivation of analytical expressions for various statistical properties, such as moments and the cumulative distribution function, of CS-RBFs as univariate and conditional densities. The approach comprises two alternative scenarios: when the CS-RBF support lies entirely within the variable's domain (untruncated support) and when part of it is outside (truncated support). Mixture models employing CS-RBFs are also analyzed, and their main properties are detailed. Furthermore, we introduce an incremental learning algorithm for density estimation with CS-RBF mixture models, in which centers are determined using k-means and weights and shape parameters are optimized by stochastic gradient descent. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that CS-RBF densities provide competitive results in terms of likelihood and model complexity in comparison with Gaussian mixture models. In addition, these CS-RBF densities allow the exact computation of key distributional properties in univariate and conditional settings.

</details>


### [160] [Coherent Measures of Discrepancy, Uncertainty and Dependence, with Applications to Bayesian Predictive Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26077)
*A. Philip Dawid*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出与任何决策问题相关的三类相干函数：不确定性、差异性和依赖性度量，并刻画其性质与相互关系。将该理论应用于贝叶斯实验选择以进行后续预测的问题，表明基于任何相干函数的相干选择准则都能得到相同解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为决策问题建立一套统一的相干度量框架，将不确定性、差异性和依赖性纳入同一理论体系，并解决贝叶斯框架下实验选择与预测问题的准则一致性。

Method: 从决策问题出发，推导出具有特殊性质的相干函数（不确定性、差异性、依赖性度量），并证明它们之间的相互决定关系；随后将理论应用于贝叶斯实验选择问题，验证基于不同相干函数的选择准则的等价性。

Result: 三类相干函数具有可刻画的特有性质，且彼此本质互定；在贝叶斯实验选择中，基于任意相干函数建立的相干选择准则均能导出相同的最优解。

Conclusion: 相干函数框架为决策问题提供了统一的理论工具，保证了在实验选择与预测问题中不同准则之间的内在一致性。

Abstract: We show how, associated with any decision problem, we may derive related functions measuring uncertainty, discrepancy and dependence of distributions. Such "coherent" functions have special properties, which we characterise, and each function essentially determines the others. The theory is applied to the Bayesian formulation of the problem of choosing an experiment in order to make a subsequent prediction. It is shown that coherent choice criteria may be based on any of the coherent functions, with related functions yielding identical solutions.

</details>


### [161] [Conditional copula representations and extremal bounds for multivariate statistical functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26256)
*Roberto Vila,Cira E G Otiniano,Carolyne Brito,Enzo Brasil*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件Copula表示方法，将期望 $\mathbb{E}[g(\boldsymbol{X})]$ 分解为边际分布与依赖结构的条件Copula贡献，构建了统一的quantile-copula框架，适用于矩、概率、依赖度量、不平等指数、熵及多元泛函等广泛统计泛函，并通过 $\Delta$-antitonicity 刻画了固定边际下的极值界，最后在风险度量、随机优势概率、信息度量和期权定价中展示了应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多元期望时通常难以清晰分离边际分布与依赖结构的影响，缺乏统一的理论框架。本文旨在通过条件Copula表示，建立一种能够显式分离两者贡献的泛函期望表达，从而统一处理各类统计泛函，并研究固定边际下依赖不确定性带来的极值界限。

Method: 推导了条件Copula表示定理，将 $\mathbb{E}[g(\boldsymbol{X})]$ 表达为边际分位数与条件Copula的积分形式；利用Copula的 concordance 序（即正象限依赖序）建立固定边际下期望的极值界；通过 $\Delta$-antitonicity 概念刻画了极值界成立的函数类条件；并给出了多个应用场景的具体实例。

Result: 成功建立了统一的条件Copula表示框架，证明了该框架可涵盖矩、概率、依赖度量、Gini系数、熵、多元泛函等；得到了固定边际下期望的上下界由 Fréchet 上下界Copula给出，且充要条件是函数满足 $\Delta$-antitonicity；在风险度量（如VaR、ES）、随机优势概率、Shannon熵、期权定价等场景下验证了框架的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的条件Copula表示为分离边际与依赖结构对期望的影响提供了统一且灵活的理论工具，拓展了Copula方法在统计泛函极值分析中的适用范围，并明确了依赖不确定性下极值界的函数特征条件，具有重要的理论价值和应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we derive a conditional copula representation for expectations of the form $\mathbb{E}[g(\boldsymbol{X})]$, where $\boldsymbol{X}$ is a random vector with arbitrary marginal distributions and $g$ is a measurable function satisfying suitable integrability conditions. The proposed representation explicitly separates the contributions of the marginal distributions and the dependence structure through conditional copula distributions, yielding a unified quantile--copula framework for a broad class of statistical functionals. This framework encompasses numerous quantities of practical interest, including moments, probabilities, dependence measures, inequality indices, entropy measures, and multivariate functionals. We further establish extremal bounds under fixed marginals by exploiting the concordance order on copulas and characterize the classes of functions for which these bounds apply through the notion of $Δ$-antitonicity. Finally, several illustrative examples illustrate the versatility of the proposed framework through applications to risk measures, stochastic superiority probabilities, information measures, and option pricing under dependence uncertainty.

</details>


### [162] [A Lower Bound on the Sliced Wasserstein Comparison Constant for Translated Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26399)
*Avery Noroozi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文推导了平移概率测度下标准1-Wasserstein距离与切片1-Wasserstein距离的闭式表达式，并构造了二者之间常数$c_d$的显式下界，揭示了切片Wasserstein距离计算效率与理论准确性之间的权衡代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切片Wasserstein距离在图像生成等应用中因计算高效而被广泛使用，但其与标准Wasserstein距离之间的理论偏差尚未充分刻画。本文旨在量化这种效率带来的理论成本。

Method: 针对$\mathbb{R}^d$上的平移概率测度，推导了$W_1$和$SW_1$的闭式表达式；并构造了Carlier等人建立的$W_1$与$SW_1$之间常数$c_d$的显式下界。

Result: 得到了平移概率测度下$W_1$和$SW_1$的解析表达式，以及常数$c_d$的一个下界。

Conclusion: 该下界揭示了切片Wasserstein距离因计算效率而付出的理论准确性代价，为理解计算效率与理论保证之间的权衡提供了定量依据。

Abstract: We provide closed-form expressions for standard 1-Wasserstein distance ($W_1$) and Sliced 1-Wasserstein distance ($SW_1$) for translated probability measures on $\mathbb{R}^d$. Furthermore, we construct an explicit lower bound for the constant $c_d$ established by Carlier, Figalli, Mérigot, and Wang between $W_1$ and $SW_1$ [1] for translated probability measures on $\mathbb{R}^d$. Sliced Wasserstein distances are computationally efficient and used in image generation and other applications, which raises natural questions in computational efficiency versus theoretical correctness. A lower bound on the constant $c_d$ gives information about the cost of that efficiency.

</details>


### [163] [Weak but Not Strong Asymptotic Testability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26476)
*Senhan Yao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文构造了两个固定的不相交平稳遍历二元过程分布族H₀和H₁，对于它们存在弱渐近一致检验，但不存在强渐近一致检验，从而反驳了Ryabko猜想中的渐近一致性分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Ryabko关于渐近一致检验存在的猜想，探究弱一致检验与强一致检验在平稳遍历过程中是否存在本质差异。

Method: 通过组合同步二元悬挂码、独立同分布标记过程和可数多个独立慢切换二态马尔可夫链来构造两个特定的分布族。

Result: 成功构造了两个分布族，使得弱渐近一致检验存在但强渐近一致检验不存在，否定了原猜想的渐近一致性分支。

Conclusion: 在平稳遍历二元过程分布中，弱渐近一致检验和强渐近一致检验之间存在不可逾越的层次差异，相关猜想需要修正。

Abstract: We construct two fixed disjoint families $H_0,H_1$ of stationary ergodic binary process distributions for which a weakly asymptotically consistent test exists, but no strongly asymptotically consistent test exists. The construction combines a synchronizing binary suspension code, an independent i.i.d marker process, and countably many independent slowly switching two-state Markov chains. In particular, this disproves the asymptotic-consistency branch of a conjecture of Ryabko.

</details>


### [164] [Hub Neighbor-Degree Diagnostics for Sparse Random Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26624)
*Qian Hui,Tiandong Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于“度k顶点的邻居平均度”的模型诊断统计量，用于区分具有相似度分布但枢纽邻域结构不同的网络模型。在秩一非均匀随机图下该统计量具有度不变中心化和k^{-1/2}波动；非秩一核下根类型后验不确定性决定中心化和尺度；线性优先附着下该统计量随(m+δ)log k增长。基于这些极限行为构建了稀疏图零模型的拟合优度检验和加权对数度斜率检验。在高中接触网络、arXiv合著网络和Reddit互动网络上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络模型可能产生几乎相同的度分布但枢纽节点（hubs）的邻域结构截然不同，缺乏有效的诊断工具来区分这类差异。本文旨在开发一种基于局部邻域统计量的模型诊断方法，能够区分不同的网络生成机制。

Method: 1) 定义“度k顶点的邻居平均度”作为诊断统计量；2) 推导该统计量在秩一非均匀随机图、非秩一核模型和线性优先附着模型下的渐近行为；3) 基于这些模型特定的极限分布构建拟合优度检验和加权对数度斜率检验；4) 通过模拟评估零假设校准、度分布误设影响以及对优先附着备择假设的检验功效。

Result: 秩一模型下统计量具有度不变中心化和k^{-1/2}波动；非秩一核下根类型不确定性改变中心化和尺度；线性优先附着下统计量随log k增长。模拟表明方法能有效区分度匹配的优先附着备择假设。实际网络分析中能分离水平误设与异配性及正残差趋势。

Conclusion: 该诊断方法能有效区分具有相似度分布但枢纽邻域结构不同的网络模型，为网络模型选择提供了一种基于局部邻域统计量的实用工具，在多个真实网络上有良好表现。

Abstract: Networks with nearly identical degree distributions can place their hubs in sharply different neighborhoods. We develop a model diagnostic based on the mean degree of the neighbors of a degree-$k$ vertex. Under rank-one inhomogeneous random graphs, this statistic has degree-invariant centering and $k^{-1/2}$ fluctuations. Under non-rank-one kernels, posterior uncertainty about the root type can instead determine both centering and scale. Under linear preferential attachment, the statistic grows as $(m+δ)\log k$. We turn these model-specific limits into goodness-of-fit tests for specified sparse-graph nulls and a weighted log-degree slope test for residual hub-neighborhood trends. Simulations evaluate null calibration, degree-distribution misspecification, and power against degree-matched preferential-attachment alternatives. Applications to high-school contact and arXiv coauthorship networks show that the method separates level misspecification from disassortative and positive residual trends. Reddit interaction networks provide a further appendix example.

</details>


### [165] [The {K}aplan--{M}eier estimator as a limit function of {E}fron's self-consistency iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27068)
*Miroslav Bacak*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了Efron在1967年提出的Kaplan-Meier估计量自一致性迭代算法的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Efron于1967年提出Kaplan-Meier估计量具有自一致性（可作为某映射的不动点），并给出了近似该不动点的迭代算法且推测其收敛，但该收敛性一直未被严格证明。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用不动点理论和迭代分析方法，严格证明Efron提出的自一致性迭代算法收敛到Kaplan-Meier估计量。

Result: 成功证明了Efron提出的自一致性迭代算法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文严格证明了Efron关于Kaplan-Meier估计量自一致性迭代算法收敛的猜想，完善了该估计量的理论基础。

Abstract: In 1967 Efron showed that the Kaplan--Meier estimator can be defined as a fixed point of a certain mapping, calling this property ``self-consistency''. He also proposed an iterative algorithm for approximating this fixed point and suggested its convergence. The purpose of the present note is to prove this fact.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Top-$k$ Pareto Bandits: Hypervolume Regret for Multi-Objective Slate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26273)
*Nicolas Gutowski,Fabien Chhel,Alexandre Letard,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机多目标博弈问题，提出THV-UCB算法，通过最大化子模函数贪婪选择k个臂来逼近Pareto前沿，并建立了gap-free和gap-dependent的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标博弈聚焦于识别单一最优臂，但实际应用中往往需要一组臂来逼近Pareto前沿。本文旨在通过维持一个小规模的臂子集来近似整个Pareto前沿，并量化这一目标的遗憾。

Method: 提出THV-UCB乐观算法，基于臂的边际超体积贡献的乐观估计进行贪婪选择。利用子模函数贪婪最大化的近似保证（1-1/e），定义α-近似超体积遗憾。

Result: 建立了无间隙遗憾界Õ(d√(nkT))和依赖于间隙的遗憾界Õ(nk²·⁵/Δ_min)，后者在臂充分分离时随T呈多对数增长。

Conclusion: 理论结果支持使用小子集逼近Pareto前沿在多种多目标应用中的有效性。

Abstract: We consider a stochastic multi-objective bandit problem where, at each round, the agent selects a slate of $k$ arms and observes their $d$-dimensional reward vectors under semi-bandit feedback. We do not aim at identifying a single optimal arm; instead, we consider the problem of maintaining a small set of actions that jointly approximate the Pareto frontier. We formalize this objective through the dominated hypervolume induced by the selected subset of arms, and define an $α$-approximate hypervolume regret with respect to the best size-$k$ subset achievable in hindsight, where $α= 1 - 1/e$ reflects the approximation guarantee of greedy maximization for monotone submodular functions. To address this problem, we introduce \textit{THV-UCB}, an optimistic algorithm that selects arms greedily based on optimistic estimates of their marginal hypervolume contributions. We establish a gap-free regret bound $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$ that holds on every instance, together with a gap-dependent bound $\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$ that becomes polylogarithmic in $T$ once the arms are sufficiently well separated. Our results provide theoretical support for using small subsets to approximate Pareto fronts in various multi-objective applications.

</details>


### [167] [High-Order Markov Blanket Discovery via a k-Order Relaxation of the Faithfulness Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26357)
*Loong Kuan Lee,Ragavi Krishnamoorthy,Nico Piatkowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种k阶忠实性假设松弛方法，并开发了kOMB算法，用于在违反忠实性假设（如XOR和奇偶校验关系）的情况下恢复变量的马尔可夫毯（MB）。实验表明，kOMB能有效处理真实分布和有限样本下的忠实性违反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习马尔可夫毯的方法普遍依赖忠实性假设，但该假设易被高阶依赖关系（如XOR、奇偶校验关系）以及有限样本下的经验违反所破坏，导致方法失效。因此，需要一种能在这类情况下仍然有效恢复MB的方法。

Method: 本文提出了忠实性假设的"k阶"松弛，专门捕获k+2个变量之间的奇偶校验类型关系；基于此松弛，设计了一个概念验证算法k阶马尔可夫毯（kOMB）用于MB发现。

Result: 实验表明，kOMB能够在真实分布违反和有限样本经验违反忠实性假设的情况下，成功恢复变量的马尔可夫毯。

Conclusion: k阶松弛的忠实性假设有效拓展了MB学习的适用范围，kOMB算法为处理高阶依赖关系下的MB发现提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The problem of learning the graphical Markov blanket (MB) of a variable from data has applications in many areas such as structure learning for Bayesian networks and Markov random fields, causal discovery, and feature selection. However, a common assumption most methods make is that the conditional independencies in the distribution imply the same separation in the graphical structure -- also known as the faithfulness assumption. Unfortunately, this assumption can be violated by higher-order dependencies such as XOR and parity-type relations, and -- on finite samples -- by empirical violations that, in extreme cases, even induce spurious dependencies absent from the true distribution. Therefore, in this paper we propose a "k-order" relaxation of the faithfulness assumption that captures parity type relationships between k+2 variables. We then propose a proof of concept algorithm called k-order Markov blanket (kOMB) that uses this relaxation for MB discovery. Finally, we empirically show how kOMB can recover the MB of a variable under both true and empirical violations of faithfulness. Code available at: https://github.com/lklee9/k-order-Markov-blanket

</details>


### [168] [Existence-Field Diffusion Model for Spatial Point Processes with Variable Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26428)
*Xiaoyin Pan,Christian R. Shelton,Rakshith Mahishi,Chengkuan Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出存在场扩散模型（EFDM），通过为每个潜在点关联存在变量，实现点过程和数量的统一扩散建模，无需离散转换操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在变基数空间点过程建模中存在局限：要么解耦基数与空间结构的建模，要么依赖离散的跨维度操作来修改点数，导致生成动力学不灵活且不对称。

Method: 提出存在场扩散模型（EFDM），为每个潜在点引入一个表示其存在程度的存在变量，从而在统一的扩散过程中联合建模空间位置和基数，无需显式的离散跨维度转换。

Result: 在可变基数的数据集上实现了更优的建模能力。

Conclusion: EFDM为空间点过程的生成建模提供了一个灵活且通用的框架。

Abstract: We study generative modeling of spatial point processes (SPP), where both the number of points and their spatial configuration are governed by a joint distribution. While diffusion models have achieved strong performance in modeling complex distributions, extending them to variable-cardinality SPP remains challenging. Existing approaches either decouple the modeling of cardinality and spatial structure, or rely on discrete trans-dimensional operations to modify the number of points, resulting in inflexible and asymmetric generative dynamics. We propose the existence-field diffusion model (EFDM) for spatial point processes modeling, where each potential point is associated with an existence variable representing its degree of presence. This enables a unified diffusion process that jointly models both spatial locations and cardinality without requiring explicit discrete transitions. We demonstrate that our approach provides a flexible and general framework for generative modeling of spatial point processes, achieving improved modeling capability on datasets with varying cardinality.

</details>


### [169] [Universality and Approximation Rates of Graph Neural Networks with Random Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26699)
*Lukas Gonon,Thilo Meyer-Brandis,Niklas Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，结合随机节点特征的排列等变神经网络（PENNs）能够以任意精度逼近任意可测的排列不变/等变函数，并给出了可微函数的逼近率上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管随机节点特征已被理论和实验证明能增强图神经网络（GNNs）的表达能力，但其在排列等变神经网络（PENNs）这一普适性框架下的通用近似性质尚未被充分理论刻画。

Method: 论文聚焦于排列等变神经网络（PENNs），这类GNN以前馈神经网络组件构建，涵盖了许多主流GNN架构。作者结合部分随机节点特征，证明在固定大小的有向图上，PENNs可在概率意义上任意逼近任意可测的排列不变/排列等变函数。对于k≥2次连续可微函数，还推导了逼近率上界，将PENNs前馈组件的复杂度（层深和非零权重数）与逼近精度相关联。

Result: 建立了PENNs结合随机节点特征的通用近似定理；推导了k次连续可微函数的逼近率上界。

Conclusion: 随机节点特征显著增强了PENNs的表达能力，使其具备通用近似能力，且逼近速率可通过网络复杂度（深度和参数数量）进行理论刻画。

Abstract: We investigate message-passing graph neural networks with random node features. Random node features are known to enhance the expressiveness of graph neural networks (GNNs) both theoretically and empirically. Here, we establish a novel universality result focusing on permutation-equivariant neural networks (PENNs), a class of GNNs built from feedforward neural network components that subsumes many prominent GNN architectures. We show that PENNs, combined with partially random node features, can approximate arbitrarily well in probability any measurable permutation-invariant or permutation-equivariant function on directed graphs of fixed size with multidimensional node and edge features. For $k$-times continuously differentiable functions, $k\geq 2$, we also derive upper bounds on the approximation rates, relating the complexity of the feedforward components of a PENN in terms of layer depth and number of nonzero weights to the desired approximation accuracy.

</details>


### [170] [BayesAME: Bayesian Active Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27023)
*Paula Cordero Encinar,Taylan Cemgil,Arnaud Doucet,Virginia Aglietti,Silvia Chiappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesAME，一个基于序贯贝叶斯框架的方法，能够自动确定评估大模型时所需的核心子集大小，在保证性能估计可靠性的前提下减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模生成模型在多个基准上的评估耗时长、计算成本高，现有方法需要人工设定核心子集大小，缺乏自动确定合适规模的机制。

Method: BayesAME采用序贯贝叶斯框架，为具有相同历史模型性能的项目组定义潜在能力变量，通过联合先验分布编码目标模型与历史模型的相似性。利用后验分布推导性能估计器、量化不确定性，并基于信息增益准则选择项目加入核心子集。迭代扩充直至性能估计波动和不确定性均低于用户定义的阈值，还提出了多目标扩展版本以捕捉多个目标模型间的性能相关性。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，BayesAME持续优于现有方法的序贯改编版本；非随机核心子集选择优于随机选择；利用连续响应对数似然比传统二元分数能显著提高估计精度。

Conclusion: BayesAME能够自动确定合适核心子集大小，在性能估计可靠性和计算效率间取得平衡，为大规模模型评估提供了实用的自动化解方案。

Abstract: Evaluating large generative models across benchmarks is time-consuming and computationally expensive. This drives the need for methods that can estimate full benchmark performance by evaluating models on only a subset of items, known as a coreset. Current literature mostly requires the practitioner to input a coreset size. However, when reliable performance estimation takes priority over efficiency, an evaluation method should also be capable of automatically determining a coreset size that reflects this priority. We introduce BayesAME, a sequential Bayesian framework specifically targeting automatic determination of the coreset size. BayesAME models performance as a random variable by defining a latent ability for each group of items sharing the same historical model performances, with a joint prior distribution encoding the belief that the target model behaves similarly to these historical models. The posterior distribution over these abilities is used to derive performance estimators, quantify performance uncertainty, and select items to add to the coreset via an information-gain criterion. The coreset is iteratively augmented until the performance estimate fluctuation and the performance uncertainty fall below their respective user-defined thresholds. We propose a multi-target extension that captures performance correlations across multiple target models to further reduce the coreset size. Through extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that BayesAME consistently outperforms sequential adaptations of existing methods. Crucially, our comprehensive analysis addresses recent skepticism in the literature, establishing that non-random coreset selection is advantageous over random selection. Finally, we highlight that leveraging continuous response log-likelihoods over traditional binary scores significantly enhances estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [171] [Emergent Sparsity in Frozen Random CNN Feature Extractors for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26059)
*Scott M. Norton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度强化学习智能体使用冻结的随机初始化CNN特征提取器时，会在第一个全连接层自发产生极度稀疏的表示（如Pong仅用1-3个活跃神经元），无需任何稀疏性诱导目标。稀疏程度与任务复杂度、随机投影的可用维度相关，且这些活跃神经元对性能至关重要。信息瓶颈在训练早期（15-30M步）就已锁定，而奖励信号要晚35-105M步才出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现并系统研究了冻结随机CNN在深度强化学习中自发产生稀疏表示的现象，揭示了随机投影与任务内在维度之间的关系。

Method: 在多个Atari游戏（Pong、Breakout、Space Invaders）上训练PPO智能体，使用冻结的随机初始化CNN，分析第一个全连接层的神经元活跃模式，通过消融实验、宽度缩放、信息瓶颈锁定时间分析等方法验证发现。

Result: FC1稀疏度随任务复杂度变化（Pong 1-11个神经元，Breakout 19-26个，Space Invaders约42个）；同一游戏不同种子产生不同稀疏度（5、7、11个），且稀疏度与最终性能相关；移除这些活跃神经元导致性能崩溃；活跃集在15-30M步锁定，远早于奖励出现（35-105M步后）。

Conclusion: 当输入维度远大于任务内在维度时，对冻结随机投影进行梯度下降可以揭示问题的有效秩，无需显式的稀疏性机制。

Abstract: We report a striking phenomenon: deep reinforcement learning agents trained with frozen, randomly initialized CNN feature extractors spontaneously develop extremely sparse fully-connected representations, without any sparsity-inducing objective. In the first fully-connected layer (FC1, $3{,}136 \to 64$), agents compress task-relevant information through as few as 1-3 neurons out of 64 for deterministic Pong (5-11 for stochastic Pong), while trainable CNNs activate 55-64 neurons under matched conditions. We establish four principal findings. First, FC1 sparsity scales with task complexity: 1-11 for Pong, 19-26 for Breakout, and $\sim$42 for Space Invaders. Width-scaling confirms this reflects task structure rather than a fixed capacity fraction. Second, within-game scaling emerges: three identical Pong seeds produce 5, 7, and 11 active neurons. The 5-neuron seed plateaus at $+14$ reward, while the others reach expert performance ($+18.4$, $+18.7$), suggesting the random projection's usable dimensionality bounds achievable performance. Third, ablation confirms necessity: removing these active neurons crashes performance across two PPO implementations and four games. Fourth, the information bottleneck commits early: a sweep shows the active set locks by 15-30M steps, while reward turns positive 35-105M steps later. A complementary finding in Breakout shows frozen and trainable CNNs reach competitive rewards via structurally different bottlenecks: frozen agents use 17-25 active neurons (participation ratio $\sim$10-14), while trainable agents use 51 (participation ratio $\sim$3.6). Finally, wherever input dimensionality dwarfs intrinsic task dimensionality, gradient descent on a frozen random projection may reveal the effective rank of the underlying problem without explicit sparsity machinery.

</details>


### [172] [Sim2Win: A Team-Agnostic, Event-Based Pre-Match Outcome Prediction and Tactical Profiling System for Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26061)
*Mouad Zemzoumi,Amine Abouaomar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sim2Win，一种与球队身份无关的、基于事件的赛前战术推荐框架。该框架利用StatsBomb开放数据，构建滚动战术档案和可解释战术特征比率，通过K-Means聚类识别八种比赛风格，并训练13个分类器预测比赛结果。在留一比赛留出（LOCO）评估中，Sim2Win在完全未见过的球队上实现了平均ROC-AUC 0.704和准确率55.4%，优于ELO、Pi-Rating和GAP基线。结果表明行为战术表征在分布偏移下具有可迁移的预测信号，是身份依赖型足球预测系统的可行替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业足球的赛前战术决策过度依赖主观专家分析和基于身份的球探系统，这些方法无法泛化到未见过的球队。现有预测系统（如ELO、Pi-Rating、GAP）依赖球队身份特征，缺乏对新对手的适应能力。因此，需要一种与球队身份无关的、可泛化的赛前战术推荐方法。

Method: Sim2Win框架包含五个步骤：（1）构建五场比赛滚动战术档案；（2）设计四个可解释的战术特征比率；（3）通过K-Means聚类将球队行为聚类为八种比赛风格；（4）从战术匹配表示中训练13个分类器（包括CatBoost等）来估计胜、平、负概率；（5）采用留一比赛留出（LOCO）评估策略，确保测试集球队在训练中完全不可见。

Result: 在涵盖178支球队、1,411个球队-比赛记录、11个比赛的数据集上，Sim2Win在LOCO评估中达到平均ROC-AUC 0.704和平均准确率55.4%，在21个ROC-AUC比较和19/21个准确率比较上优于ELO、Pi-Rating和GAP基线。CatBoost在分布内表现最强，准确率达60.90%。

Conclusion: 行为战术表征在分布偏移下能提供可迁移的预测信号，Sim2Win是身份依赖型足球预测系统的有效替代方案，证明了不依赖球队身份的战术分析在比赛结果预测中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Pre-match tactical decision-making in professional football relies heavily on subjective expert analysis and identity-based scouting systems that cannot generalize to unseen teams. This paper presents Sim2Win, a team-agnostic, event-based pre-match tactical recommendation framework that reframes match outcome prediction as a tactical decision-support problem. Using StatsBomb open event data from eleven competitions spanning 178 teams and 1,411 team-match records, Sim2Win constructs five-match rolling tactical profiles, engineers four interpretable tactical feature ratios, clusters team behaviors into eight playstyles via K-Means, and trains thirteen classifiers to estimate win, draw, and loss probabilities from tactical matchup representations.
  The system operates without team names or identity features, enabling generalization to teams never seen during training. A rigorous Leave-One-Competition-Out (LOCO) evaluation demonstrates that Sim2Win achieves a mean ROC-AUC of 0.704 and mean accuracy of 55.4% on completely unseen teams, outperforming ELO, Pi-Rating, and GAP baselines on all 21 ROC-AUC comparisons and 19 of 21 accuracy comparisons. Among all evaluated models, CatBoost achieved the strongest in-distribution performance with 60.90% accuracy.
  These findings suggest that behavioral tactical representations provide transferable predictive signal under distribution shift and offer a viable alternative to identity-dependent football prediction systems.

</details>


### [173] [Meta-Learned Reward Shaping for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26094)
*Yunpeng Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MeRLa框架，通过元学习任务感知的奖励塑形函数，在RLHF训练前学习辅助任务，生成复合奖励以提供任务特定学习信号，同时保持策略最优性。在LLaMA-3-8B上，MeRLa在AlpacaEval 2.0上达到90.8%的长度控制胜率，MT-Bench得分9.14，训练不稳定性降低41%，显著优于PPO、DPO、GRPO和DAPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF使用静态、任务无关的奖励模型，导致稀疏学习信号和次优对齐，无法适应不同任务的特定需求。

Method: 提出MeRLa框架，在RLHF训练前通过元学习在辅助任务上学习一个任务感知的塑形函数Φ(x,y;φ)，生成复合奖励。元目标函数结合了任务判别、熵正则化和基于势的守恒约束，保证了策略不变性并提供了任务特定的学习信号。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B上，AlpacaEval 2.0长度控制胜率90.8%，MT-Bench得分9.14，训练不稳定性降低41%，在四项基准测试中一致优于PPO、DPO、GRPO和DAPO。

Conclusion: MeRLa通过元学习奖励塑形有效解决了RLHF中奖励模型静态和任务无关的问题，提供了理论保证（策略不变性、表示漂移敏感性分析、激励对齐），并能与基于过程和基于评分的增强奖励兼容。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard approach for aligning large language models with human preferences, but its quality is limited by static, task-agnostic reward models. This mismatch leads to sparse learning signals and suboptimal alignment. We introduce MeRLa (Meta-Learned Reward Shaping), a principled framework that meta-learns a task-aware shaping function $Φ(x,y;φ)$ across auxiliary tasks before RLHF training. The learned shaping produces a composite reward that preserves policy optimality while providing task-specific learning signals. Our meta-objective combines task discrimination, entropy regularization, and potential-based conservation for stable convergence. We provide theoretical guarantees for policy invariance, analyze representation drift sensitivity, and formally address incentive misalignment from entropy maximization. Experiments on LLaMA-3-8B across four benchmarks show consistent improvements over PPO, DPO, GRPO, and DAPO, achieving a 90.8% length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2.0 and a score of 9.14 on MT-Bench, with 41% less training instability. MeRLa retains its benefits when combined with process-based and rubric-based enhanced rewards.

</details>


### [174] [Data Fusion and Contrastive Alignment for Unconstrained IR Molecular Structure Elucidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26164)
*Ethan J. Mick,Campbell A. Sweet,Matthias J. Young,Derek T. Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了对传统编码器-解码器Transformer的关键改进，包括基于Choquet积分的非加性聚合的混合专家（MoE）解码器模块和对比对齐损失，使Top-K预测准确率提升超过10个百分点，显著增强了从红外光谱进行自动化分子结构解析的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从红外光谱进行分子结构解析的方法依赖预定义的化学式作为辅助输入，只能进行异构体识别而非完整结构预测；无约束场景下Transformer模型的可靠性较低且理解不足。

Method: 1) 提出基于线性阶统计量和Choquet积分的非加性聚合的MoE解码器模块；2) 在光谱表示聚合中也采用非加性算子；3) 引入辅助的对比对齐损失项。

Result: 相比基线红外光谱模型，Top-K预测准确率提升超过10个百分点；通过子结构片段分析证实红外光谱编码了绝大部分相关化学信息。

Conclusion: 证明了从实测红外光谱进行自动化分子结构解析的有效性，显著扩展了AI在分析化学中的实用性。

Abstract: Automated molecular structure elucidation from infrared (IR) spectroscopy data has seen significant advancements in recent years, but its broad applicability is limited by a reliance on pre-determined chemical formulas provided as auxiliary model inputs. This limits model predictions to isomer identification rather than full molecular structure prediction. Although transformer models have been shown to identify molecular isomers with high accuracy, their reliability for unconstrained structure elucidation is comparatively low and poorly understood. In this work, we propose and evaluate key modifications to the traditional encoder-decoder transformer. To better address the vast chemical space of the unconstrained problem, we implement a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoder module that utilizes non-additive aggregation via linear-order statistics and the Choquet integral. We further modify the transformer to utilize these non-additive operators when aggregating spectral representations as well. Together with an auxiliary contrastive alignment loss term, these enhancements improve Top-K prediction accuracy by over 10 percentage points compared to baseline IR-only models. Through sub-structure fragment analysis of molecular predictions, we further confirm that infrared spectra encode the vast majority of relevant chemical information, implying that the higher performance of isomer-ranking models is largely due to underrepresented or overlapping absorption bands for molecules in the explored chemical space. Ultimately, by demonstrating the efficacy of automated molecular structure elucidation from measured IR spectra, this work serves to significantly broaden the utility of AI in analytical chemistry.

</details>


### [175] [Shared SFT Lessons Across Alignment, Model Organisms, and Toy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26173)
*Anton de la Fuente,Arthur Conmy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了监督微调（SFT）在三个不同研究领域（对齐训练、模式生物和玩具模型）之间的知识迁移。通过三个实验，验证了从一个领域学到的SFT经验可以迁移到其他领域：1）将行为泛化经验从对齐训练迁移到玩具模型；2）将能力保持经验从模式生物迁移到对齐设置；3）将鲁棒性经验从模式生物迁移到对齐设置。研究表明跨领域借用SFT技术可以提升所有相关领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐训练、模式生物和玩具模型通常被视为独立的研究领域，但它们都使用监督微调（SFT）追求相同目标。当项目共享目标时，应检验从一个领域学到的经验是否能迁移到其他领域。

Method: 设计了三个跨领域迁移实验：1）将对齐训练中"行为原因训练"（Teaching Claude Why）的经验迁移到玩具模型，对比基于原因训练与仅基于示例训练的行为泛化效果；2）将模式生物中"离策略数据能力损伤"的经验迁移到Model-Spec Midtraining对齐设置，测试混合良性在策略数据是否能减少损伤；3）将模式生物中"鲁棒性"经验迁移到同一对齐设置，测试后续良性SFT是否会擦除对齐行为。

Result: 1）基于原因训练比基于示例训练更能促进行为泛化；2）混合良性在策略数据可以防止离策略数据造成的大部分能力损伤，同时保留目标行为；3）后续良性SFT会擦除对齐行为但保留能力，表明仅能力保持不足以确保对后续训练的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨领域借用SFT经验可以提升所有相关研究领域，建议更多研究者从自身领域之外借鉴技术。

Abstract: Alignment training, model organisms, and toy models are usually treated as separate research areas. But projects in all three frequently use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to pursue the same underlying goals. When projects share a goal, we should test whether lessons learned from one area transfer to the other areas. We study three such transfers, each taking a lesson developed in one SFT setting and testing it in another. First, we port a lesson about behavior generalization from alignment training into toy models. Training on the reason for a behavior, as in Teaching Claude Why, can make the behavior generalize better than training on examples of the behavior alone. Second, we port a lesson about capability preservation from model organisms into the Model-Spec Midtraining alignment setting. SFT on outputs written by a model other than the student (off-model outputs) can damage capabilities when trained on. Mixing in benign on-model (and on-policy) data into our training can prevent most of this damage while still embedding the target behavior. Third, we port a lesson about robustness from model organisms into the same alignment setting. We find that follow-up benign SFT can erase the alignment behavior while preserving capabilities, showing that capability preservation alone does not ensure robustness to subsequent training. Our work illustrates how porting SFT lessons between different research fields can uplift them all, suggesting more researchers should borrow techniques from outside their own areas.

</details>


### [176] [Dynamic Parameterization Is Not Dynamic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26192)
*Zongfei Li,Yuan-yih Shang,Guozhong Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“冻结控制器审计”（FCA）方法，通过缓存未扰动轨迹上的完整系数张量、禁用控制器并重放冻结模型（包括跨输入重分配、token洗牌和静态剖面估计），来区分动态参数化的三种不同性质：系数变化、冻结模型对系数分配的依赖、以及条件执行。实验表明，静态层剖面保留了98.70%-99.43%的性能差距，层身份解释了87%-96%的系数方差，但FeatureGate和MUDDPythia都执行了所有Transformer块，推断速度反而比Dense慢30.8%。结论：动态参数化本身不代表动态推理，功能动态也不代表计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献常将输入依赖的控制器系数视为动态推理或计算节省的证据，但混淆了系数变化、冻结模型对系数分配的依赖、条件执行这三种不同性质。作者希望厘清这些概念，并提出一个通用原则来分别衡量这些性质。

Method: 提出冻结控制器审计（FCA）框架：先沿着未扰动轨迹缓存完整系数张量，然后禁用控制器，用缓存的系数进行重放。重放时进行跨输入重分配、token洗牌和使用独立校准集估计的静态剖面。由于系数在干预前已缓存，性能变化仅反映分配依赖性，而不会受到扰动隐状态重新计算控制器的影响。

Result: 在7个独立训练的76M FeatureGate Transformers和3个504M模型上：静态层剖面分别保留了98.70%和99.43%的Correct-to-GlobalMean性能差距；层身份解释了87%-96%的系数方差；FeatureGate执行所有Transformer块，推断比Dense慢30.8%。在MUDDPythia-1.4B上：跨输入重分配和token洗牌分别使NLL增加1.9067和2.9637；MUDDPythia也执行所有Transformer块。

Conclusion: 动态参数化本身不建立动态推理，功能动态不建立计算节省。关于动态模型的报告应分开报告：系数变化、冻结模型的功能依赖性、以及实际执行情况。

Abstract: Input-dependent controller coefficients are often treated as evidence of dynamic inference or computational savings. This interpretation conflates three properties: coefficient variation, dependence of a frozen model on how coefficients are assigned to inputs, and conditional execution. We focus on the second property and formulate a general principle of frozen-controller auditing. We provide one concrete implementation, Frozen-Controller Auditing (FCA), which caches the complete coefficient tensor along an unperturbed trajectory, disables the controller, and replays the frozen model with cross-input reassignment, token shuffling, and static profiles estimated from an independent calibration set. Because the coefficients are cached before any intervention, performance changes under replay measure assignment dependence without feedback from recomputing the controller on perturbed hidden states. Across seven independently trained 76M FeatureGate Transformers and three 504M models, static layerwise profiles retain 98.70% and 99.43% of the Correct-to-GlobalMean performance gap, respectively. Layer identity explains 87% to 96% of the coefficient variance. FeatureGate nevertheless executes every Transformer block, and its measured inference is 30.8% slower than Dense. On the public MUDDPythia-1.4B checkpoint, cross-input reassignment and token shuffling increase NLL by 1.9067 and 2.9637, respectively. These penalties show that the model depends strongly on content-conditioned cross-layer assignment. MUDDPythia also executes every Transformer block.
